---

A checkbox allows the user to select one or more options from a number of choices.

```tsx
import { Checkbox, CheckboxGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const CheckboxExample: FC = () => {
  const [selection, setSelection] = useState<string[]>(['option-1']);

  return (
    <CheckboxGroup value={selection} onValueChange={setSelection}>
      <Checkbox value="option-1">Option 1</Checkbox>
      <Checkbox value="option-2">Option 2</Checkbox>
      <Checkbox value="option-3">Option 3</Checkbox>
    </CheckboxGroup>
  );
};

export default CheckboxExample;
```

## Import

Import `Checkbox` to use the component.

```ts
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox`:

### Checkbox

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isChecked` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the checkbox is checked. |
| `isIndeterminate` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the checkbox is indeterminate. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the checkbox is disabled. |
| `isReadonly` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the checkbox is read-only. |
| `value` | `string` | - | No | The value associated with this checkbox. |
| `onCheckedChange` | `(isChecked: boolean) => void` | - | No | Emits whenever the selection state changes. |
| `defaultChecked` | `boolean` | - | No | The default checked state of the checkbox. This is only used when the component is uncontrolled. |

### CheckboxGroup

The `CheckboxGroup` component is a container for multiple `Checkbox` components.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `value` | `string[]` | `[]` | No | The currently selected checkboxes. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | `false` | No | Disables all checkboxes in the group. |
| `isReadonly` | `boolean` | `false` | No | Makes all checkboxes in the group read-only. |
| `onValueChange` | `(value: string[]) => void` | - | No | Emits whenever the selection state changes. |

## Examples

### Standalone

The checkbox can be used standalone (i.e. without a group).

```tsx
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const CheckboxStandaloneExample: FC = () => {
  const [checked, setChecked] = useState(false);

  return (
    <Checkbox isChecked={checked} onCheckedChange={setChecked}>
      Accept terms and conditions
    </Checkbox>
  );
};

export default CheckboxStandaloneExample;
```

---

Chips, also known as chiclets or tags, are a visual representation for applied filter or search criteria, or for other sorts of selected values. A chip contains a label and, if applicable a remove icon which removes the value or criteria from the selection.

```tsx
import { Chip, ChipAvatar, ChipColorTag } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import { ArchiveIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const ChipExample: FC = () => (
  <div
    style={{
      display: 'flex',
      gap: '40px',
      alignItems: 'center',
    }}
  >
    <Chip size="medium">Value</Chip>
    <Chip size="medium" icon={<ArchiveIcon />}>
      Value
    </Chip>
    <Chip size="medium" icon={<ChipAvatar color="dark-blue">OT</ChipAvatar>}>
      Value
    </Chip>
    <Chip size="medium" icon={<ChipAvatar image="./assets/avatar.png" />}>
      Value
    </Chip>
    <Chip size="medium" icon={<ChipColorTag color="#6055F1" />}>
      Value
    </Chip>
  </div>
);

export default ChipExample;
```

## Import

Import `Chip` to use the component.

```ts
import { Chip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip`:

### Chip

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `size` | `'medium' | 'large'` | `large` | No | The size of the chip. |
| `isSelectable` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the chip can be selected. |
| `isSelected` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the chip is selected. |
| `onSelectedChange` | `(selected: boolean) => void` | - | No | Emitted when the user selects the chip. |
| `isRemovable` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the chip can be removed. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the chip is disabled. |
| `isReadonly` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the chip is readonly. |
| `onRemove` | `() => void` | - | No | Emitted when the user removes the chip. |
| `icon` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Icon, avatar or color tag to display in the chip. |

### ChipColorTag

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `color` | `string` | - | No | Define the CSS color within the component. |

### ChipAvatar

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `color` | `string` | - | No | Background color of the avatar when there is no image. This should be the name of a color from the color palette. |
| `image` | `string` | - | No | Image source for the avatar. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Chip component in different scenarios.

### Colors tags

The Chip component can be displayed with different colors.

```tsx
import { Chip, ChipColorTag, ChipTagColor } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const ChipColorsExample: FC = () => {
  const colors: ChipTagColor[] = [
    '#6055F1',
    'var(--jato-color-teal-1720)',
    'var(--jato-color-blue-3365)',
    'var(--jato-color-discovery-4235)',
  ];

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        gap: '4px',
        alignItems: 'center',
      }}
    >
      {colors.map(stat => (
        <Chip size="medium" key={stat} icon={<ChipColorTag color={stat} />}>
          Value
        </Chip>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
};

export default ChipColorsExample;
```

### Selectable

The Chip component can be configured to be selectable when using chips for filtering by turning on/off the displayed value.

```tsx
import { Chip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const ChipSelectableExample: FC = () => {
  const [chip1Selected, setChip1Selected] = useState(false);
  const [chip2Selected, setChip2Selected] = useState(true);

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        gap: '4px',
        alignItems: 'center',
      }}
    >
      <Chip
        size="medium"
        isSelectable
        isSelected={chip1Selected}
        onSelectedChange={setChip1Selected}
      >
        Value
      </Chip>
      <Chip
        size="medium"
        isSelectable
        isSelected={chip2Selected}
        onSelectedChange={setChip2Selected}
      >
        Value
      </Chip>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ChipSelectableExample;
```

### Removable

The Chip component can be configured to allow the user to remove a chip.

```tsx
import { Chip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const ChipRemovableExample: FC = () => {
  const [showChip1, setShowChip1] = useState(true);
  const [showChip2, setShowChip2] = useState(true);

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        gap: '4px',
        alignItems: 'center',
      }}
    >
      {showChip1 && (
        <Chip size="medium" isRemovable onRemove={() => setShowChip1(false)}>
          Value
        </Chip>
      )}
      {showChip2 && (
        <Chip size="medium" isRemovable onRemove={() => setShowChip2(false)}>
          Value
        </Chip>
      )}
    </div>
  );
};

export default ChipRemovableExample;
```

### Draggable

The chip component can be used within a draggable list. There are a number of drag and drop libraries available
that can be used to achieve this functionality. The example below uses the `@dnd-kit` libraries to create a draggable list of chips.

```tsx
import { DndContext, DragEndEvent, DragOverlay, DragStartEvent } from '@dnd-kit/core';
import {
  SortableContext,
  useSortable,
  horizontalListSortingStrategy,
  arrayMove,
} from '@dnd-kit/sortable';
import { Chip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import { AlarmClockIcon, BroadcastIcon, CalendarIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { ArchiveIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/micro-focus';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const ChipDraggableExample: FC = () => {
  const [activeItem, setActiveItem] = useState<ChipItem | null>(null);
  const [chips, setChips] = useState<ChipItem[]>([
    { id: 1, icon: <ArchiveIcon />, label: 'Archive' },
    { id: 2, icon: <AlarmClockIcon />, label: 'Time' },
    { id: 3, icon: <CalendarIcon />, label: 'Date' },
    { id: 4, icon: <BroadcastIcon />, label: 'Broadcast' },
  ]);

  function handleDragStart({ active }: DragStartEvent): void {
    setActiveItem(chips.find(chip => chip.id === active.id) ?? null);
  }

  function handleDragEnd({ active, over }: DragEndEvent): void {
    if (over && active.id !== over.id) {
      setChips(items => {
        const oldIndex = items.findIndex(item => item.id === active.id);
        const newIndex = items.findIndex(item => item.id === over.id);

        return arrayMove(items, oldIndex, newIndex);
      });
    }

    setActiveItem(null);
  }

  return (
    <DndContext onDragStart={handleDragStart} onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}>
      <SortableContext items={chips} strategy={horizontalListSortingStrategy}>
        <div
          style={{
            display: 'flex',
            gap: 12,
            alignItems: 'center',
          }}
        >
          {chips.map(chip => (
            <DraggableChip key={chip.id} id={chip.id} icon={chip.icon} label={chip.label} />
          ))}
        </div>
      </SortableContext>

      <DragOverlay>
        {activeItem ? (
          <Chip size="medium" icon={activeItem.icon} data-dragging={true}>
            {activeItem.label}
          </Chip>
        ) : null}
      </DragOverlay>
    </DndContext>
  );
};

interface ChipItem {
  id: number;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
}

function DraggableChip({ id, icon, label }: ChipItem): React.ReactNode {
  const { attributes, listeners, setNodeRef, transform } = useSortable({
    id,
  });

  const style = {
    transform: `translateX(${transform?.x ?? 0}px) translateY(${transform?.y ?? 0}px)`,
  };

  return (
    <Chip size="medium" icon={icon} ref={setNodeRef} style={style} {...attributes} {...listeners}>
      {label}
    </Chip>
  );
}

export default ChipDraggableExample;
```

---

Coachmarks are overlays that can help explain features in the user interface. They contain concise text tips and help links. They can be used individually or in a short chain of multiple coachmarks. They are most effective when focussing on new features or less visible but useful options. The user can dismiss coachmarks and once closed they do not appear again.

```tsx
import { Button, IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  Coachmark,
  CoachmarkChain,
  CoachmarkContent,
  CoachmarkFooter,
  CoachmarkHeader,
  CoachmarkLink,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/coachmark';
import { AddIcon, FilterIcon, TreeViewIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef, useState } from 'react';

const CoachmarkExample: FC = () => {
  const triggerRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(true);

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column' }}>
      <div>
        <Button primary isDisabled={isOpen} onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}>
          Launch coachmark
        </Button>
      </div>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <IconButton ref={triggerRef} key="add" flat icon={<AddIcon />} aria-label="action" />
        <IconButton
          key="filter"
          flat
          icon={<FilterIcon />}
          aria-label="action"
          aria-describedby="coachmark1"
        />
        <IconButton key="notification" flat icon={<TreeViewIcon />} aria-label="action" />
      </div>
      <CoachmarkChain isOpen={isOpen} onOpenChange={setIsOpen}>
        <Coachmark
          id="coachmark1"
          anchorRef={triggerRef}
          position="below"
          alignment="start"
          aria-labelledby="coachmark1"
        >
          <CoachmarkHeader> Feature title</CoachmarkHeader>
          <CoachmarkContent>
            A very brief description or instruction about the feature or function. Use coachmarks
            sparingly. Display coachmarks right after a new dialog or page is opened.
          </CoachmarkContent>
          <CoachmarkFooter>
            <CoachmarkLink href="javascript:void(0)">Learn more about Feature title</CoachmarkLink>
          </CoachmarkFooter>
        </Coachmark>
      </CoachmarkChain>
    </div>
  );
};

export default CoachmarkExample;
```

## Import

Import `Coachmark` to use the component.

```ts
import { Coachmark } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/coachmark';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/coachmark`:

### Coachmark Chain

This component is a container for `Coachmark` components when the users wants to add multiple coachmarks.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isOpen` | `boolean` | `true` | No | Whether coachmarks should be presented to the user. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(isOpen: boolean) => void` | - | No | Event emitted when the open state of the coachmarks changes. |

### Coachmark

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `id` | `string` | - | Yes | The unique identifier for the coachmark. |
| `position` | `above | below | before | after` | `above` | No | The position of the coachmark relative to the trigger element. |
| `alignment` | `start | center | end` | `center` | No | The alignment of the coachmark relative to the trigger element. |
| `anchorRef` | `RefObject` | - | Yes | The element that the coachmark is anchored to. |
| `offset` | `number` | - | No | The offset of the coachmark from the trigger element. |

### CoachmarkHeader

This component is a container for the title and dismiss button.

### CoachmarkContent

This component is a container for main text content of the coachmark.

### CoachmarkLink

This component can be used as a link within the coachmark.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `href` | `string` | - | - | The url to open on click. |

### CoachmarkFooter

This component is a container for the navigation buttons.

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Coachmarks component in different scenarios.

### Single coachmark (see example above)

Single coachmarks can be shown to provide a tip on a specific element. For example: for a new feature shown after sign-in; for a new function in a modal when the modal is opened; and for a specific addition to a menu when it is opened.
Do not use single coachmarks if multiple tips should be given on a single page or modal. Instead, use a coachmark chain with a Next button so that the user can dismiss the complete chain and does not have to dismiss multiple single coachmarks.

### Coachmark chain

A coachmark chain can be shown when the user first opens a page, dialog or menu to provide tips on different elements. The Next button proceeds through each coachmark in the chain.
A click on the dismiss icon closes the coachmarks and ends the chain. The chain does not start again, even if the user ended it before the last coachmark.

```tsx
import { Button, IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  Coachmark,
  CoachmarkChain,
  CoachmarkContent,
  CoachmarkFooter,
  CoachmarkHeader,
  CoachmarkLink,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/coachmark';
import { AddIcon, FilterIcon, TreeViewIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef, useState } from 'react';

const CoachmarkChainExample: FC = () => {
  const triggerRef1 = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const triggerRef2 = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const triggerRef3 = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);

  return (
    <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', alignItems: 'center' }}>
      <Button primary isDisabled={isOpen} onClick={() => setIsOpen(!isOpen)}>
        Launch coachmark chain
      </Button>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', justifyContent: 'center' }}>
        <IconButton
          ref={triggerRef1}
          key="add"
          flat
          icon={<AddIcon />}
          aria-label="action"
          aria-describedby="coachmark2"
        />
        <IconButton
          ref={triggerRef2}
          key="filter"
          flat
          icon={<FilterIcon />}
          aria-label="action"
          aria-describedby="coachmark1"
        />
        <IconButton
          ref={triggerRef3}
          key="notification"
          flat
          icon={<TreeViewIcon />}
          aria-label="action"
        />
      </div>

      <CoachmarkChain isOpen={isOpen} onOpenChange={setIsOpen}>
        <Coachmark
          id="coachmark1"
          key="coachmark1"
          anchorRef={triggerRef1}
          position="below"
          alignment="start"
          aria-labelledby="coachmark1"
        >
          <CoachmarkHeader> Feature title</CoachmarkHeader>
          <CoachmarkContent>
            A very brief description or instruction about the feature or function. Use coachmarks
            sparingly. Display coachmarks right after a new dialog or page is opened.
          </CoachmarkContent>
          <CoachmarkFooter>
            <CoachmarkLink href="javascript:void(0)">Learn more about Feature title</CoachmarkLink>
          </CoachmarkFooter>
        </Coachmark>

        <Coachmark
          id="coachmark2"
          key="coachmark2"
          anchorRef={triggerRef2}
          position="below"
          alignment="center"
          aria-labelledby="coachmark2"
        >
          <CoachmarkHeader> Feature title</CoachmarkHeader>
          <CoachmarkContent>
            A very brief description or instruction about the feature or function. Use coachmarks
            sparingly. Display coachmarks right after a new dialog or page is opened.
          </CoachmarkContent>
          <CoachmarkFooter>
            <CoachmarkLink href="javascript:void(0)">Learn more about Feature title</CoachmarkLink>
          </CoachmarkFooter>
        </Coachmark>
        <Coachmark
          id="coachmark3"
          key="coachmark3"
          anchorRef={triggerRef3}
          position="below"
          alignment="end"
          aria-labelledby="coachmark3"
        >
          <CoachmarkHeader> Feature title</CoachmarkHeader>
          <CoachmarkContent>
            A very brief description or instruction about the feature or function. Use coachmarks
            sparingly. Display coachmarks right after a new dialog or page is opened.
          </CoachmarkContent>
          <CoachmarkFooter>
            <CoachmarkLink href="javascript:void(0)">Learn more about Feature title</CoachmarkLink>
          </CoachmarkFooter>
        </Coachmark>
      </CoachmarkChain>
    </div>
  );
};
export default CoachmarkChainExample;
```

## Localization

This component uses several strings which can be localized. You can use `CoachmarkI18nContext` to configure these strings.

```tsx
import { CoachmarkI18nContext } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/breadcrumbs';

<CoachmarkI18nContext.Provider value={{ closeButtonAriaLabel: "Bouton de fermeture", ... }}>
  <CoachmarkChain />
</CoachmarkI18nContext.Provider>
```

The following strings are available for localization:

| Name                     | Description                          | Type     |
| ------------------------ | ------------------------------------ | -------- |
| **closeButtonAriaLabel** | The aria label for the close button. | `string` |

---

The combo box allows users to make a selection from a pre-populated list of options or type their own input.

```tsx
import { Combobox, SelectOption } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const ComboboxExample: FC = () => {
  const optionList = ['Option 1', 'Option 2', 'Option 3'];
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>();

  return (
    <Combobox<string>
      value={value}
      onValueChange={setValue}
      placeholder="Type or select an option"
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
    >
      {optionList.map(option => {
        return (
          <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
            {option}
          </SelectOption>
        );
      })}
    </Combobox>
  );
};

export default ComboboxExample;
```

## Import

Import `Combobox` to use the component.

```ts
import { Combobox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/select`:

### Combobox

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `trigger` | `ComboboxTrigger` | `input` | - | The trigger that will open the dropdown. |
| `size` | `small | medium | large` | `medium` | - | The size of the Combobox component. |
| `onFilterChange` | `(value: string) => void` | - | - | Emits whenever the filter text changes. |
| `value` | `string | T` | - | - | Value of the control. |
| `placeholder` | `string | ReactNode` | - | - | Text to show when no value is selected. |
| `dropdownPlacement` | `auto | top | bottom` | `auto` | - | Where the dropdown should be aligned relative to the control. By default, the dropdown will open below if there is enough space, otherwise it will open above. |
| `isOpen` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether the dropdown is visible. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(isOpen: boolean) => void` | - | - | Emitted when the user opens or closes the options panel. |
| `valueRender` | `(value: string | T) => ReactNode` | - | - | Define a custom renderer for the selected value. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether the control is disabled. |
| `onValueChange` | `(value: string | T) => void` | - | - | Emitted when the user modifies the value. |
| `maxHeight` | `string | number` | - | - | The max height of the option panel. Accepts a number of pixels, or any CSS value with units. |
| `clearable` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether the select should show a clear button. |
| `ariaLabel` | `string` | - | - | Specifies the ARIA label for the combobox component. |
| `ariaLabelledBy` | `string` | - | - | Specifies the ARIA labelledby for the combobox component. |
| `displayWith` | `(value: string | T) => string` | - | - | Function used to transform values to display strings. |
| `defaultValue` | `string | T` | - | - | The initial value of the component. |
| `panelWidth` | `string | number` | - | - | The width of the dropdown panel. |
| `onScrolledToEnd` | `() => void` | - | - | Emitted when the user scrolls to the end of the list. |
| `container` | `HTMLElement` | - | No | The container element the combobox panel will be appended to. This defaults to the document body. |

## Examples

### Size

The combobox component supports three sizes: small, medium and large.

```tsx
import { Combobox, SelectOption } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const ComboboxSizesExample: FC = () => {
  const optionList = ['Option 1', 'Option 2', 'Option 3'];
  const [valueSmall, setValueSmall] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const [valueMedium, setValueMedium] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const [valueLarge, setValueLarge] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        width: '300px',
        gap: 16,
      }}
    >
      <Combobox<string>
        value={valueSmall}
        onValueChange={setValueSmall}
        size="small"
        placeholder="Small size variant"
      >
        {optionList.map(option => {
          return (
            <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
              {option}
            </SelectOption>
          );
        })}
      </Combobox>

      <Combobox<string>
        value={valueMedium}
        onValueChange={setValueMedium}
        size="medium"
        placeholder="Medium size variant"
      >
        {optionList.map(option => {
          return (
            <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
              {option}
            </SelectOption>
          );
        })}
      </Combobox>

      <Combobox<string>
        value={valueLarge}
        onValueChange={setValueLarge}
        size="large"
        placeholder="Large size variant"
      >
        {optionList.map(option => {
          return (
            <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
              {option}
            </SelectOption>
          );
        })}
      </Combobox>
    </div>
  );
};

export default ComboboxSizesExample;
```

### Clearable

The combobox component has the option to add a clear button.

```tsx
import { Combobox, SelectOption } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const ComboboxClearableExample: FC = () => {
  const optionList = ['Option 1', 'Option 2', 'Option 3'];
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>(optionList[0]);

  return (
    <Combobox<string>
      value={value}
      onValueChange={setValue}
      placeholder="Type or select an option"
      clearable
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
    >
      {optionList.map(option => {
        return (
          <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
            {option}
          </SelectOption>
        );
      })}
    </Combobox>
  );
};

export default ComboboxClearableExample;
```

### Search

The combobox component can be configured to select an option via search input.

```tsx
import { Combobox, SelectFeedback, SelectOption } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const ComboboxSearchExample: FC = () => {
  const [options] = useState([
    'Marty McFly',
    'Emmett Brown',
    'Biff Tannen',
    'Jennifer Parker',
    'George McFly',
    'Lorraine Baines',
  ]);
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string>();
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<string>('');

  const results = useMemo(
    () => options.filter(option => option.toLowerCase().includes(filter!.toLowerCase())),
    [options, filter]
  );

  return (
    <Combobox<string>
      value={value}
      onValueChange={setValue}
      onFilterChange={setFilter}
      aria-label="combobox input"
      placeholder="Type or select an option"
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
    >
      {results.length > 0 ? (
        results.map(option => (
          <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
            {option}
          </SelectOption>
        ))
      ) : (
        <SelectFeedback>No results</SelectFeedback>
      )}
    </Combobox>
  );
};

export default ComboboxSearchExample;
```

### Option Group Headings

The combobox component supports option group headings.

```tsx
import {
  Combobox,
  SelectOption,
  SelectGroup,
  SelectGroupHeader,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const ComboboxGroupExample: FC = () => {
  const groupsList: SelectOptionGroup[] = [
    {
      title: 'Group 1',
      options: ['Option 1', 'Option 2', 'Option 3'],
    },
    {
      title: 'Group 2',
      options: ['Option 4', 'Option 5', 'Option 6'],
    },
  ];

  return (
    <Combobox<string> placeholder="Type or select an option" style={{ width: '300px' }}>
      {groupsList.map(group => (
        <SelectGroup key={group.title}>
          <SelectGroupHeader>{group.title}</SelectGroupHeader>
          {group.options.map(option => (
            <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
              {option}
            </SelectOption>
          ))}
        </SelectGroup>
      ))}
    </Combobox>
  );
};

interface SelectOptionGroup {
  title: string;
  options: string[];
}

export default ComboboxGroupExample;
```

### Load more on scroll

The select component supports loading more options when the user scrolls to the bottom of the list using the `onScrolledToEnd` event.

```tsx
import { Combobox, SelectOption } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const ComboboxLoadMoreExample: FC = () => {
  const [options, setOptions] = useState(Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => `Option ${i + 1}`));
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const loadNextPage = async (): Promise<void> => {
    // simulate a delay of 1/2 second
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));

    const nextPage = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => `Option ${currentPage * 20 + i + 1}`);
    setOptions([...options, ...nextPage]);
    setCurrentPage(currentPage + 1);
  };

  return (
    <Combobox
      placeholder="Type or select an option"
      onScrolledToEnd={loadNextPage}
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
    >
      {options.map(option => (
        <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
          {option}
        </SelectOption>
      ))}
    </Combobox>
  );
};

export default ComboboxLoadMoreExample;
```

---

A date picker allows the specifying of a date via a calendar flyout or by text entry.

```tsx
import { Button, IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  DatePicker,
  DatePickerPopover,
  useDatePickerInput,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/date-picker';
import { FormField, FormFieldLabel } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/form';
import { CalendarIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { InputGroup, TrailingAddon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/input-group';
import { TextInput } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input';
import { DateTime } from 'luxon';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const dateFormat = 'dd MMMM yyyy';

const DatePickerExample: FC = () => {
  const {
    setDate,
    datePickerProps,
    inputGroupProps,
    textInputProps,
    toggleButtonProps,
    popoverProps,
  } = useDatePickerInput({
    date: DateTime.utc(2026, 3, 18),
    toDateString: date => date.toFormat(dateFormat),
    toDate: value => {
      const date = DateTime.fromFormat(value, dateFormat);
      return date.isValid ? date : null;
    },
  });

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        justifyContent: 'center',
      }}
    >
      <FormField style={{ width: 244 }}>
        <FormFieldLabel>Select a date</FormFieldLabel>
        <InputGroup
          {...inputGroupProps}
          trailingAddon={
            <TrailingAddon>
              <IconButton
                {...toggleButtonProps}
                icon={<CalendarIcon />}
                size="small"
                aria-label="Toggle date picker"
              />
            </TrailingAddon>
          }
        >
          <TextInput autoComplete="off" {...textInputProps} />
        </InputGroup>

        <DatePickerPopover {...popoverProps}>
          <DatePicker<DateTime> {...datePickerProps} />
        </DatePickerPopover>
      </FormField>
    </div>
  );
};

export default DatePickerExample;
```

## Import

Import `DatePicker` and `DatePickerPopover` to use the component.

```ts
import { DatePicker, DatePickerPopover } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/date-picker';
```

**Note**: This component requires a [date time adapter](/react/get-started/date-time-adapters) to be configured before use.

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/date-picker`:

### DatePicker

The date picker component. This can be used within a `DatePickerPopover` or standalone.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `value` | `TDateTime` | - | No | The value for the default Date. |
| `min` | `TDateTime` | - | No | The minimum range value for the calendar. |
| `max` | `TDateTime` | - | No | The maximum range value for the calendar. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the control is disabled. |
| `isDateDisabled` | `(date: TDateTime) => boolean` | - | No | Whether a specific date is disabled. |
| `previousMonthBtnAriaLabel` | `string` | - | No | Aria label for previous month button. |
| `nextMonthBtnAriaLabel` | `string` | - | No | Aria label for next month button. |
| `startOfWeek` | `StartOfWeek` | `7` | No | The index of the first day of the calendar week. Values range from 1 (Monday) to 7 (Sunday). |
| `closeOnSelect` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the date picker popup should close after selection. |
| `onValueChange` | `(value: TDateTime) => void` | - | No | Emit when the selected date changes. |

### DatePickerPopover

A component that extends the `Popover` component to provide the recommended layout for date pickers and accompanying controls.

---

Flyout panels help to display additional information of an object and may provide simple actions without having to navigate to a different location.

[UXD guidelines](https://uxd.opentext.net/guidelines/flyout_panels.htm)

## Import

Import `Flyout` to use the component.

```ts
import { Flyout } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/popover';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/popover`:

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isOpen` | `boolean` | - | - | Determine if the Flyout is open or closed. |
| `placement` | `above | below | before | after` | `below` | - | The placement of the Flyout in relation to the trigger element. Defaults to 'below'. |
| `alignment` | `start | center | end` | `center` | - | The alignment of the Flyout in relation to the trigger element. Defaults to 'center'. |
| `variant` | `auto | light | dark` | `auto` | - | The color variant of the Flyout. |
| `triggerRef` | `RefObject` | - | Yes | The element used to trigger Flyout. The Flyout will be positioned relative to this element. |
| `openTriggers` | `click | mouseenter | focus` | `['click']` | - | Events that will cause the Flyout to appear. |
| `closeTriggers` | `click | mouseleave | blur | escape | clickoutside | mouseoutside` | `['clickoutside' | 'escape']` | - | Events that will cause the Flyout to hide. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(isOpen: boolean) => void` | - | - | Emit whenever the Flyout is opened or closed. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | - | Determine if the Flyout is disabled. |
| `offset` | `number` | - | - | The offset of the Flyout from the trigger element. |
| `delay` | `number` | - | - | The delay before the Flyout is shown in milliseconds. |
| `container` | `HTMLElement` | - | - | The container element the flyout will be appended to. This defaults to the document body. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Flyout component with different configurations.

### Placement

The Flyout component supports four placement options: above, below, before, and after.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { Flyout, Placement, useFlyoutTrigger } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/popover';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const FlyoutButton: FC<{ label: string; placement: Placement }> = ({ label, placement }) => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const { flyoutProps, triggerProps } = useFlyoutTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>({
    isOpen,
    onOpenChange: setIsOpen,
  });

  return (
    <>
      <Button primary {...triggerProps}>
        {label}
      </Button>
      <Flyout {...flyoutProps} placement={placement}>
        <span>Flyout {placement}</span>
      </Flyout>
    </>
  );
};

const FlyoutPlacementExample: FC = () => {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: 40,
      }}
    >
      <FlyoutButton label="Above" placement="above" />
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '150px' }}>
        <FlyoutButton label="Before" placement="before" />
        <FlyoutButton label="After" placement="after" />
      </div>
      <FlyoutButton label="Below" placement="below" />
    </div>
  );
};

export default FlyoutPlacementExample;
```

### Alignment

The Flyout component supports three alignment options: start, center, and end.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { Alignment, Flyout, useFlyoutTrigger } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/popover';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const FlyoutButton: FC<{ label: string; alignment: Alignment }> = ({ label, alignment }) => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const { flyoutProps, triggerProps } = useFlyoutTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>({
    isOpen,
    onOpenChange: setIsOpen,
  });

  return (
    <>
      <Button primary {...triggerProps}>
        {label}
      </Button>
      <Flyout {...flyoutProps} alignment={alignment}>
        <span>Flyout aligned to {alignment}</span>
      </Flyout>
    </>
  );
};

const FlyoutAlignmentExample: FC = () => {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        alignItems: 'center',
      }}
    >
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '40px' }}>
        <FlyoutButton label="Start" alignment="start" />
        <FlyoutButton label="Center" alignment="center" />
        <FlyoutButton label="End" alignment="end" />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
};

export default FlyoutAlignmentExample;
```

Forms and form controls provide layout, labels, and feedback to facilitate data entry and validation.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { Form, FormActionGroup, FormField, FormFieldLabel } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/form';
import { TextInput } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const FormExample: FC = () => {
  return (
    <Form style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 340 }}>
      <FormField>
        <FormFieldLabel isRequired>Name</FormFieldLabel>
        <TextInput placeholder="Enter full name" type="text" />
      </FormField>

      <FormField>
        <FormFieldLabel isRequired>Email</FormFieldLabel>
        <TextInput placeholder="Enter email address" type="email" />
      </FormField>

      <FormActionGroup>
        <Button primary type="submit">
          Submit
        </Button>
        <Button secondary type="reset">
          Reset
        </Button>
      </FormActionGroup>
    </Form>
  );
};

export default FormExample;
```

## Import

Import the following components to create a form.

```ts
import { Form, FormField, FormFieldLabel, FormActionGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/form';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/form`:

### Form

The `Form` is a container for form fields.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `orientation` | `'horizontal' | 'vertical'` | `vertical` | No | Layout of the form, default is 'vertical'. |
| `labelsColumnWidth` | `string` | - | No | Width of the labels column in the horizontal orientation. CSS length units. |
| `controlsColumnWidth` | `string` | - | No | Width of the controls column in the horizontal orientation. CSS length units. |

### FormField

The `FormField` is a container for form controls, the associated label, and any feedback.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isValid` | `boolean` | `true` | No | Whether the field is valid. |

### FormFieldLabel

This should be applied to a label element to associate it with a form field. The label should have a `for` attribute that matches the `id` of the form field.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isRequired` | `boolean` | `false` | No | Whether to show the required indicator. |

### FormActionGroup

The `FormActionGroup` is a container for form actions such as submit and cancel buttons.

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Form component in different scenarios.

### Validation

The following example demonstrates how to use the form component with validation. The React form components
work with most popular form libraries, such as Formik and React Hook Form.

```tsx
import { zodResolver } from '@hookform/resolvers/zod';
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  Form,
  FormActionGroup,
  FormField,
  FormFieldFeedback,
  FormFieldLabel,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/form';
import { ErrorSmallIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato-status';
import { TextInput } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';
import { SubmitHandler, useForm } from 'react-hook-form';
import { z } from 'zod';

const schema = z.object({
  name: z.string().min(1, 'The name field is required.'),
  email: z.string().min(1, 'The email field is required.').email('The email address is invalid.'),
});

type FormInput = z.infer<typeof schema>;

const FormValidationExample: FC = () => {
  const {
    register,
    handleSubmit,
    formState: { errors },
  } = useForm<FormInput>({
    mode: 'onBlur',
    resolver: zodResolver(schema),
  });

  const onSubmit: SubmitHandler<FormInput> = data => {
    // handle successful form data here
    console.log(data);
  };

  return (
    <Form style={{ width: '100%', maxWidth: 340 }} onSubmit={handleSubmit(onSubmit)}>
      <FormField isValid={!errors.name}>
        <FormFieldLabel isRequired>Name</FormFieldLabel>
        <TextInput placeholder="Enter full name" type="text" {...register('name')} />

        <FormFieldFeedback whenInvalid icon={<ErrorSmallIcon />}>
          {errors.name?.message}
        </FormFieldFeedback>
      </FormField>

      <FormField isValid={!errors.email}>
        <FormFieldLabel isRequired>Email</FormFieldLabel>
        <TextInput placeholder="Enter email address" type="email" {...register('email')} />
        <FormFieldFeedback whenInvalid icon={<ErrorSmallIcon />}>
          {errors.email?.message}
        </FormFieldFeedback>
      </FormField>

      <FormActionGroup>
        <Button primary type="submit">
          Submit
        </Button>
        <Button secondary type="reset">
          Reset
        </Button>
      </FormActionGroup>
    </Form>
  );
};

export default FormValidationExample;
```

---

The Global Header (masthead) provides the logo, product identity and access to global features.

```tsx
import { Avatar } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/avatar';
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  AviatorPrimaryIcon,
  HelpCircleIcon,
  MenuIcon,
  MoreCircleIcon,
  NotificationIcon,
  SearchIcon,
  StarIcon,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { OpentextSmallLogo } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/logo';
import {
  Masthead,
  MastheadTitle,
  MastheadTitleSecondary,
  MastheadVersion,
  MyAviatorButton,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/masthead';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const GlobalHeaderExample: FC = () => (
  <Masthead
    logo={<OpentextSmallLogo />}
    menuButton={<IconButton flat primary icon={<MenuIcon />} aria-label="menu button" />}
    title={
      <MastheadTitle>
        Primary name
        <MastheadTitleSecondary>Secondary name</MastheadTitleSecondary>
        <MastheadVersion>24.1</MastheadVersion>
      </MastheadTitle>
    }
    actions={[
      <IconButton key="search" flat icon={<SearchIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
      <IconButton key="aviator" flat icon={<AviatorPrimaryIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
      <IconButton key="notification" flat icon={<NotificationIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
      <IconButton key="star" flat icon={<StarIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
      <IconButton key="help" flat icon={<HelpCircleIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
      <IconButton key="more" flat icon={<MoreCircleIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
    ]}
    avatar={<Avatar initials="AA" />}
    myAviator={<MyAviatorButton>MyAviator</MyAviatorButton>}
  />
);

export default GlobalHeaderExample;
```

## Import

Import `Masthead` to use the component.

```ts
import {
  Masthead,
  MastheadTitle,
  MastheadVersion,
  SecondaryMasthead,
  MastheadModule,
  MastheadTab,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react`:

### Masthead

Used a container for the logo, title, version, and tabs.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `icon` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the icon slot of the masthead. |
| `tabs` | `ReactNode | ReactNode[]` | - | No | Content to render in the tabs slot of the masthead. |
| `actions` | `ReactNode | ReactNode[]` | - | No | Content to render in the actions slot of the masthead. |
| `title` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the title slot of the masthead. |
| `breadcrumbs` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the breadcrumbs slot of the masthead. |
| `menuButton` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the menuButton slot of the masthead. |
| `logo` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the logo slot of the masthead. |
| `module` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the module slot of the masthead. |
| `avatar` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the avatar slot of the masthead. |
| `myAviator` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the myAviator slot of the masthead. |

### MastheadTitle

Used to define the title of the application. The title should be provided as a child of the component.
An `href` prop can be used to provide a link to the title, in which case the title will be rendered as an anchor tag.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `is` | `string` | `h1` | No | Name of the element type to render the masthead title with. |
| `variant` | `'primary' | 'secondary'` | `primary` | No | The variant of the masthead title. |

### MastheadVersion

Used to define the version of the application. The version number should be provided as a child of the component.

### SecondaryMasthead

Used to define the secondary level of the global header. This typically contains module name, tabs and actions.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `tabs` | `ReactNode[]` | - | No | Content to render in the tabs slot of the masthead. |
| `module` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Content to render in the module slot. |
| `actions` | `ReactNode[]` | - | No | Content to render in the actions slot. |

### MastheadModule

Used to define the module name in the global header. The module name should be provided as a child of the component.

### MastheadTab

Used to define a tab in the global header. This is typically used to navigate between different sections of the application.

The tab can be used with an `href` attribute to navigate to a different route.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the tab is disabled. |
| `isSelected` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the tab is selected. |

### MyAviatorButton

Used to add a MyAviator button to the global header.

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Global Header component in different scenarios.

### Secondary Global Header

The secondary global header is used to define the module name, tabs and actions.

```tsx
import { Avatar } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/avatar';
import { Button, IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  AviatorPrimaryIcon,
  HelpCircleIcon,
  MenuIcon,
  MoreCircleIcon,
  MoreHorizontalIcon,
  NotificationIcon,
  SearchIcon,
  StarIcon,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { OpentextSmallLogo } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/logo';
import {
  Masthead,
  MastheadModule,
  MastheadTab,
  MastheadTitle,
  MastheadTitleSecondary,
  MastheadVersion,
  MyAviatorButton,
  SecondaryMasthead,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/masthead';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const GlobalHeaderSecondaryExample: FC = () => (
  <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', width: '100%' }}>
    <Masthead
      logo={<OpentextSmallLogo />}
      menuButton={<IconButton flat primary icon={<MenuIcon />} aria-label="menu button" />}
      title={
        <MastheadTitle>
          Primary name
          <MastheadTitleSecondary>Secondary name</MastheadTitleSecondary>
          <MastheadVersion>24.1</MastheadVersion>
        </MastheadTitle>
      }
      actions={[
        <IconButton key="search" flat icon={<SearchIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
        <IconButton key="aviator" flat icon={<AviatorPrimaryIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
        <IconButton key="notification" flat icon={<NotificationIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
        <IconButton key="star" flat icon={<StarIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
        <IconButton key="help" flat icon={<HelpCircleIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
        <IconButton key="more" flat icon={<MoreCircleIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
      ]}
      avatar={<Avatar initials="AA" />}
      myAviator={<MyAviatorButton>MyAviator</MyAviatorButton>}
    />
    <SecondaryMasthead
      module={<MastheadModule>Module name</MastheadModule>}
      tabs={[
        <MastheadTab key="overview" isSelected>
          Overview
        </MastheadTab>,
        <MastheadTab key="dashboard">Dashboard</MastheadTab>,
      ]}
      actions={[
        <Button key="button1" primary>
          Button
        </Button>,
        <Button key="button2" secondary>
          Button
        </Button>,
        <IconButton key="button3" secondary icon={<MoreHorizontalIcon />} aria-label="action" />,
      ]}
    />
  </div>
);

export default GlobalHeaderSecondaryExample;
```

---

A hamburger menu offers navigation to major locations or features within an application or app suite.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { DrawerContainer } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/drawer';
import {
  ComplianceIcon,
  DashboardIcon,
  DocumentIcon,
  HamburgerCloseIcon,
  LaunchIcon,
  MenuIcon,
  SettingsIcon,
  StarIcon,
  UserIcon,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { OpentextLogo } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/logo';
import { Masthead } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/masthead';
import {
  NavigationDrawer,
  NavigationHeader,
  NavigationMinimizeButton,
  NavigationTree,
  NavigationTreeItem,
  useNavigationTreeControl,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/navigation';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const HamburgerMenuExample: FC = () => {
  const [minimized, setMinimized] = useState(false);
  const items = useMemo<NavigationTreeItem[]>(
    () => [
      {
        title: 'Dashboard',
        icon: <DashboardIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Products',
        icon: <LaunchIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Report',
        icon: <DocumentIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Alerts',
        icon: <ComplianceIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Favorites',
        icon: <StarIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Settings',
        icon: <SettingsIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Account',
        icon: <UserIcon />,
      },
    ],
    []
  );

  const treeControl = useNavigationTreeControl({ items });

  return (
    <DrawerContainer
      style={{ width: 'calc(100vw - 32px)', display: 'block' }}
      drawer={
        <NavigationDrawer
          mode="overlay"
          isMinimized={minimized}
          onMinimizedChange={setMinimized}
          minimizeOnOutsideClick
        >
          <NavigationHeader
            minimizeButton={
              <NavigationMinimizeButton aria-label="minimize button">
                <HamburgerCloseIcon />
              </NavigationMinimizeButton>
            }
          >
            <OpentextLogo />
          </NavigationHeader>

          <NavigationTree treeControl={treeControl} />
        </NavigationDrawer>
      }
    >
      <Masthead
        menuButton={
          <IconButton
            flat
            primary
            icon={<MenuIcon />}
            onClick={() => setMinimized(false)}
            aria-label="menu button"
          />
        }
      />
      {/* Content goes here */}
    </DrawerContainer>
  );
};

export default HamburgerMenuExample;
```

## Import

```ts
import {
  NavigationDrawer,
  NavigationHeader,
  NavigationMinimizeButton,
  NavigationSearch,
  NavigationTree,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/navigation';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/navigation`:

### NavigationDrawer

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isMinimized` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the drawer is minimized or not. |
| `onMinimizedChange` | `(isMinimized: boolean) => void` | - | No | Emit when the drawer's minimized state changes. |

### DrawerContainer

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `drawer` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Where the drawer is placed inside the drawer container. |

### NavigationHeader

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `children` | `ReactNode` | - | No | The content to render in the header. |
| `minimizeButton` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Define a minimize button. |

### NavigationMinimizeButton

A wrapper around the `Button` component that provides will minimize the drawer when clicked.

### NavigationSearch

A container for a search input field.

### NavigationTree

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isItemActive` | `(item: NavigationTreeItem
) => boolean` | - | No | A function which determines whether a navigation item is currently active. |
| `node` | `FC
>` | - | No | Custom component used to render navigation items. |
| `treeControl` | `UxaTreeControl
` | - | Yes | Provides data and can be used to programmatically expand/collapse nodes in the tree. |
| `expandOn` | `'chevron' | 'item'` | `item` | No | Whether the items are expanded by clicking on the item or chevron. Defaults to `item`. |
| `onNavigate` | `(item: NavigationTreeItem
) => void` | - | No | Emits when activating a navigation item (click or keypress). |
| `loadChildren` | `(node: NavigationTreeItem
) => Promise` | - | No | Load children asynchronously. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Hamburger Menu component in different scenarios.

### Nested Navigation

The following example demonstrates a nested navigation tree.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { DrawerContainer } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/drawer';
import {
  CloseSmallIcon,
  ComplianceIcon,
  DashboardIcon,
  DocumentIcon,
  HamburgerCloseIcon,
  LaunchIcon,
  MenuIcon,
  SearchSmallIcon,
  SettingsIcon,
  StarIcon,
  UserIcon,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import {
  InputGroup,
  SearchButton,
  SearchClearButton,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/input-group';
import { OpentextLogo } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/logo';
import { Masthead } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/masthead';
import {
  NavigationDrawer,
  NavigationHeader,
  NavigationMinimizeButton,
  NavigationSearch,
  NavigationTree,
  NavigationTreeItem,
  useNavigationTreeControl,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/navigation';
import { TextInput } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const HamburgerMenuNestedExample: FC = () => {
  const [minimized, setMinimized] = useState(false);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');

  const items = useMemo<NavigationTreeItem[]>(
    () => [
      {
        title: 'Dashboard',
        icon: <DashboardIcon />,
        children: [{ title: 'Sales' }],
      },
      {
        title: 'Products',
        icon: <LaunchIcon />,
        children: [
          {
            title: 'Add Product',
            children: [{ title: 'Add Product 1' }, { title: 'Add Product 2' }],
          },
          { title: 'Edit Product' },
          { title: 'Delete Product' },
        ],
      },
      {
        title: 'Report',
        icon: <DocumentIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Alerts',
        icon: <ComplianceIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Favorites',
        icon: <StarIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Settings',
        icon: <SettingsIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Account',
        icon: <UserIcon />,
      },
    ],
    []
  );

  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    const query = search.toLowerCase();

    const filterItems = (options: NavigationTreeItem[]): NavigationTreeItem[] => {
      return options
        .map(item => {
          const matchedChildren = item.children ? filterItems(item.children) : [];
          const matches = item.title.toLowerCase().includes(query) || matchedChildren.length > 0;

          if (matches) {
            return { ...item, children: matchedChildren };
          }
          return null;
        })
        .filter(item => item !== null) as NavigationTreeItem[];
    };

    return filterItems(items);
  }, [items, search]);

  const treeControl = useNavigationTreeControl({ items: filteredItems });

  return (
    <DrawerContainer
      style={{ width: 'calc(100vw - 32px)', display: 'block' }}
      drawer={
        <NavigationDrawer mode="overlay" isMinimized={minimized} onMinimizedChange={setMinimized}>
          <NavigationHeader
            minimizeButton={
              <NavigationMinimizeButton aria-label="minimize button">
                <HamburgerCloseIcon />
              </NavigationMinimizeButton>
            }
          >
            <OpentextLogo />
          </NavigationHeader>

          <NavigationSearch>
            <InputGroup
              trailingAddon={
                <>
                  <SearchClearButton icon={<CloseSmallIcon />} />
                  <SearchButton icon={<SearchSmallIcon />} />
                </>
              }
            >
              <TextInput
                placeholder="Search"
                type="search"
                value={search}
                onValueChange={setSearch}
              />
            </InputGroup>
          </NavigationSearch>

          <NavigationTree treeControl={treeControl} />
        </NavigationDrawer>
      }
    >
      <Masthead
        menuButton={
          <IconButton
            flat
            primary
            icon={<MenuIcon />}
            onClick={() => setMinimized(false)}
            aria-label="menu button"
          />
        }
      />
      {/* Content goes here */}
    </DrawerContainer>
  );
};

export default HamburgerMenuNestedExample;
```

### No Icons

The following example demonstrates a navigation tree without icons, and a navigation item in an active state.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { DrawerContainer } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/drawer';
import { HamburgerCloseIcon, MenuIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { OpentextLogo } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/logo';
import { Masthead } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/masthead';
import {
  NavigationDrawer,
  NavigationHeader,
  NavigationMinimizeButton,
  NavigationTree,
  NavigationTreeItem,
  useNavigationTreeControl,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/navigation';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const HamburgerMenuNoIconsExample: FC = () => {
  const [minimized, setMinimized] = useState(false);

  const items = useMemo<NavigationTreeItem[]>(
    () => [
      {
        title: 'Dashboard',
        children: [{ title: 'Sales' }],
      },
      {
        title: 'Products',
        children: [
          {
            title: 'Add Product',
            children: [{ title: 'Add Product 1' }, { title: 'Add Product 2' }],
          },
          { title: 'Edit Product' },
          { title: 'Delete Product' },
        ],
      },
      { title: 'Report' },
      { title: 'Alerts' },
      { title: 'Favorites' },
      { title: 'Settings' },
      { title: 'Account' },
    ],
    []
  );

  const treeControl = useNavigationTreeControl({ items });

  const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] = useState<NavigationTreeItem | null>(items[4]); // Set a navigation item as initially active

  return (
    <DrawerContainer
      style={{ width: 'calc(100vw - 32px)', display: 'block' }}
      drawer={
        <NavigationDrawer mode="overlay" isMinimized={minimized} onMinimizedChange={setMinimized}>
          <NavigationHeader
            minimizeButton={
              <NavigationMinimizeButton aria-label="minimize button">
                <HamburgerCloseIcon />
              </NavigationMinimizeButton>
            }
          >
            <OpentextLogo />
          </NavigationHeader>

          <NavigationTree treeControl={treeControl} isItemActive={item => item === selectedItem} />
        </NavigationDrawer>
      }
    >
      <Masthead
        menuButton={
          <IconButton
            flat
            primary
            icon={<MenuIcon />}
            onClick={() => setMinimized(false)}
            aria-label="menu button"
          />
        }
      />
      {/* Content goes here */}
    </DrawerContainer>
  );
};

export default HamburgerMenuNoIconsExample;
```

---

The Icon Button component is a derivative of the Button component for icon-only buttons. These take up less space and can be command (raised) or subtle (flat) and are available in primary and secondary styles.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { NotificationIcon, ShareIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/micro-focus';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const IconButtonVariantsExample: FC = () => (
  <div
    style={{
      display: 'flex',
      justifyContent: 'center',
      alignItems: 'center',
      gap: 16,
    }}
  >
    <IconButton primary icon={<NotificationIcon />} aria-label="icon button" />
    <IconButton secondary icon={<ShareIcon />} aria-label="icon button" />
  </div>
);

export default IconButtonVariantsExample;
```

## Import

Import `IconButton` to use the component.

```ts
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/button`:

### IconButton

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `icon` | `ReactNode` | - | - | The icon to display. |
| `primary` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether it's a primary button. |
| `secondary` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether it's a secondary button. |
| `size` | `small | medium | large` | - | - | The size of the button. |
| `flat` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether it's a flat button. |
| `isCircular` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether the button has a circular appearance. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | `false` | - | Whether the button is disabled. This prevents user interactions and enables disabled behaviour and styling for buttons. |
| `ariaDisabled` | `boolean` | - | - | Define the aria disabled state of the button. This maintains readability and focusability for screenreaders. |
| `href` | `string` | - | - | The URL to navigate to when the button is clicked. If provided, the button will be rendered as an anchor element. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Button component in different scenarios.

### Variants

The Icon Button component supports the color variants primary and secondary.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { NotificationIcon, ShareIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/micro-focus';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const IconButtonVariantsExample: FC = () => (
  <div
    style={{
      display: 'flex',
      justifyContent: 'center',
      alignItems: 'center',
      gap: 16,
    }}
  >
    <IconButton primary icon={<NotificationIcon />} aria-label="icon button" />
    <IconButton secondary icon={<ShareIcon />} aria-label="icon button" />
  </div>
);

export default IconButtonVariantsExample;
```

### Size

The Icon Button component supports three sizes: small, medium, and large.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { NotificationIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/micro-focus';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const IconButtonSizesExample: FC = () => (
  <div
    style={{
      display: 'flex',
      justifyContent: 'center',
      alignItems: 'center',
      gap: 16,
    }}
  >
    <IconButton primary size="small" icon={<NotificationIcon />} aria-label="icon button" />
    <IconButton primary size="medium" icon={<NotificationIcon />} aria-label="icon button" />
    <IconButton primary size="large" icon={<NotificationIcon />} aria-label="icon button" />
  </div>
);

export default IconButtonSizesExample;
```

### Subtle Icon Buttons

The icon buttons also support a subtle appearance. All the variants and sizes are available for subtle buttons.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { NotificationIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/micro-focus';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const IconSubtleButtonVariantsExample: FC = () => (
  <IconButton primary flat icon={<NotificationIcon />} aria-label="icon button" />
);

export default IconSubtleButtonVariantsExample;
```

### Link Buttons

The icon buttons can be used as links by setting the `href` prop.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { NotificationIcon, ShareIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/micro-focus';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const IconButtonLinksExample: FC = () => (
  <div
    style={{
      display: 'flex',
      justifyContent: 'center',
      alignItems: 'center',
      gap: 16,
    }}
  >
    <IconButton
      primary
      icon={<NotificationIcon />}
      href="javascript:void(0)"
      aria-label="icon button"
    />
    <IconButton secondary icon={<ShareIcon />} href="javascript:void(0)" aria-label="icon button" />
  </div>
);

export default IconButtonLinksExample;
```

Icons help create a consistent visual language across products, while being recognizable, scalable, and accessible.

```tsx
import {
  AcknowledgeIcon,
  LaunchIcon,
  NotificationIcon,
  OrganizationChartIcon,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const IconExample: FC = () => {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        gap: 16,
        padding: 8,
        fontSize: 24,
      }}
    >
      <NotificationIcon />
      <LaunchIcon />
      <AcknowledgeIcon />
      <OrganizationChartIcon />
    </div>
  );
};

export default IconExample;
```
