---

The multi-value chip is a variant of the chip component allowing multiple values to be shown in the same chip.

```tsx
import { MultiValueChip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const MultiValueChipExample: FC = () => {
  const [values, setValues] = useState<string[]>([
    'Video',
    'Document',
    'Photo',
    'PDF',
    'Wiki page',
    'Perspective',
    'Web report',
    'Workflow',
    'Search query',
    'Social Media Post',
    'Video Script',
    'User Guide',
    'Article',
    'Podcast Episode',
    'Landing Page',
    'Webinar',
    'White Paper',
    'Ebook',
    'Tutorial',
    'Webinar',
    'Product Description',
    'Press Release',
    'Template',
    'Workspace',
    'FAQ Section',
    'Research Report',
    'User Feedback Form',
    'Infographic',
    'Checklist',
    'Resource List',
    'Slide Deck',
    'Interactive Quiz',
    'Survey',
    'Case Study',
    'Comparison Chart',
    'Blog Post',
    'News Update',
  ]);
  return (
    <MultiValueChip
      label="Content type"
      values={values}
      onValuesChange={setValues}
      style={{ maxWidth: 312 }}
    />
  );
};

export default MultiValueChipExample;
```

## Import

Import `MultiValueChip` to use the component.

```ts
import { MultiValueChip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip`:

### MultiValueChip

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `label` | `string` | - | Yes | The chip label. |
| `values` | `readonly string[]` | - | No | The selected values. |
| `isExpanded` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the component is expanded. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the component is disabled. |
| `isReadonly` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the component is readonly. |
| `onExpandedChange` | `EventEmitter` | - | No | Emit when the component is expanded. |
| `onValuesChange` | `EventEmitter` | - | No | Emit when the values have changed. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Multi Value Chip component in different scenarios.

### Single Value

The multi-value chip can be used to display a single value.

```tsx
import { MultiValueChip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const MultiValueChipSingleExample: FC = () => {
  const [values, setValues] = useState<string[]>(['Document']);

  return values.length ? (
    <MultiValueChip
      label="Content type"
      values={values}
      onValuesChange={setValues}
      style={{ maxWidth: 312 }}
    />
  ) : null;
};

export default MultiValueChipSingleExample;
```

### Readonly

The multi-value chip can be set to readonly to prevent the user from removing values.

```tsx
import { MultiValueChip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const MultiValueChipReadonlyExample: FC = () => {
  const [values, setValues] = useState<string[]>([
    'Document',
    'Wiki page',
    'Perspective',
    'PDF',
    'Photo',
    'Web report',
    'Workflow',
    'Workspace',
    'Search query',
  ]);

  return values.length ? (
    <MultiValueChip
      label="Content type"
      values={values}
      onValuesChange={setValues}
      isReadonly
      style={{ maxWidth: 312 }}
    />
  ) : null;
};

export default MultiValueChipReadonlyExample;
```

### Disabled

The multi-value chip can be set to disabled to prevent the user from interacting with the component.

```tsx
import { MultiValueChip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const MultiValueChipDisabledExample: FC = () => {
  const [values] = useState<string[]>([
    'Document',
    'Wiki page',
    'Perspective',
    'PDF',
    'Photo',
    'Web report',
    'Workflow',
    'Workspace',
    'Search query',
  ]);

  return (
    <MultiValueChip label="Content type" values={values} isDisabled style={{ maxWidth: 312 }} />
  );
};

export default MultiValueChipDisabledExample;
```

## Localization

This component uses several strings which must be localized. You can use `MultiValueChipI18nContext` to configure these strings.

```tsx
import { MultiValueChipI18nContext } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/chip';

<MultiValueChipI18nContext.Provider
  value={{
    footerButtonLabel: 'Effacer',
    clearButtonLabel: 'Clair',
  }}
>
  <MultiValueChip />
</MultiValueChipI18nContext.Provider>;
```

The following strings are available for localization:

| Name                  | Description                                                                    | Type     |
| --------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------ | -------- |
| **footerButtonLabel** | The label for the button in the footer of the expanded multi-value chip modal. | `string` |
| **clearButtonLabel**  | The label for the button to clear a single value chip.                         | `string` |

---

This is a coachmark for specific use with the MyAviator shortcut on the global header.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  MyAviatorCoachmark,
  MyAviatorCoachmarkContent,
  MyAviatorCoachmarkFeature,
  MyAviatorCoachmarkSubtitle,
  MyAviatorCoachmarkTitle,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/coachmark';
import {
  MenuIcon,
  NotesIcon,
  PodcastIcon,
  TranslateIcon,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { MyAviatorIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato-illustration';
import { OpentextSmallLogo } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/logo';
import {
  Masthead,
  MastheadTitle,
  MastheadTitleSecondary,
  MastheadVersion,
  MyAviatorButton,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/masthead';
import { NewTag } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/tags';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef, useState } from 'react';

export const MyAviatorCoachmarkExample: FC = () => {
  const anchorRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const [showCoachmark, setShowCoachmark] = useState(true);

  return (
    <div style={{ width: '100%' }}>
      <Masthead
        logo={<OpentextSmallLogo />}
        menuButton={<IconButton flat primary icon={<MenuIcon />} aria-label="menu button" />}
        title={
          <MastheadTitle>
            Primary name
            <MastheadTitleSecondary>Secondary name</MastheadTitleSecondary>
            <MastheadVersion>24.1</MastheadVersion>
          </MastheadTitle>
        }
        myAviator={
          <MyAviatorButton ref={anchorRef} onClick={() => setShowCoachmark(true)}>
            MyAviator
          </MyAviatorButton>
        }
      />

      <MyAviatorCoachmark
        anchorRef={anchorRef}
        isOpen={showCoachmark}
        onDismiss={() => setShowCoachmark(false)}
        illustration={<MyAviatorIcon />}
        tag={<NewTag>New</NewTag>}
        title={<MyAviatorCoachmarkTitle>Introducing OpenText MyAviator</MyAviatorCoachmarkTitle>}
      >
        <MyAviatorCoachmarkContent>
          <MyAviatorCoachmarkSubtitle>
            A secure AI personal assistant built to simplify everyday work.
          </MyAviatorCoachmarkSubtitle>

          <MyAviatorCoachmarkFeature
            icon={<NotesIcon />}
            header="Summarize files instantly"
            description="Get quick, clear summaries of long documents - no more reading through everything."
          />

          <MyAviatorCoachmarkFeature
            icon={<TranslateIcon />}
            header="Translate documents in a click"
            description="Convert documents into other languages, accurately and quickly."
          />

          <MyAviatorCoachmarkFeature
            icon={<PodcastIcon />}
            header="Generate Podcasts"
            description="Turn documents into engaging podcasts."
          />
        </MyAviatorCoachmarkContent>
      </MyAviatorCoachmark>
    </div>
  );
};

export default MyAviatorCoachmarkExample;
```

## Import

Import the My Aviator Coachmark components.

```ts
import {
  MyAviatorCoachmark,
  MyAviatorCoachmarkContent,
  MyAviatorCoachmarkFeature,
  MyAviatorCoachmarkTitle,
  MyAviatorCoachmarkSubtitle,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/coachmark';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/coachmark`:

### MyAviatorCoachmark

The container component for the coachmark content.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `anchorRef` | `RefObject` | - | Yes | The element to anchor the coachmark to. |
| `isOpen` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the coachmark is open. |
| `onDismiss` | `() => void` | - | No | Called when the coachmark is dismissed. |
| `tag` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Display a tag at the top of the coachmark. |
| `illustration` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Display an illustration in the coachmark. |
| `title` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Display a title in the coachmark. |
| `dismissButtonAriaLabel` | `string` | - | No | The dismiss button aria-label. |

### MyAviatorCoachmarkContent

The content region for the coachmark. This region becomes scrollable when the content exceeds the available space.

### MyAviatorCoachmarkFeature

Call out important features with a header, icon, and description.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `icon` | `ReactNode` | - | Yes | The icon to display for the feature. |
| `header` | `string` | - | Yes | The header to display for the feature. |
| `description` | `string` | - | Yes | The description to display for the feature. |

### MyAviatorCoachmarkTitle

The title to display for the coachmark.

### MyAviatorCoachmarkSubtitle

The subtitle to display for the coachmark.

## Responsiveness

The coachmark is fully responsive, automatically adjusting its layout for various screen sizes. The illustration scales down on smaller viewports and is hidden if vertical space is limited, ensuring the content remains accessible. On very small screens, the title may be truncated to maximize space for essential information.

## Localization

This component uses strings which should be localized. You can use `MyAviatorCoachmarkI18nContext` function to configure these strings.

```tsx
import { MyAviatorCoachmarkI18nContext } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/coachmark';

<MyAviatorCoachmarkI18nContext.Provider value={{ dismissButtonAriaLabel: "Le dismiss button", ... }}>
  <MyAviatorCoachmark />
</MyAviatorCoachmarkI18nContext.Provider>
```

The following strings are available for localization:

| Name                       | Description                            | Type     |
| -------------------------- | -------------------------------------- | -------- |
| **dismissButtonAriaLabel** | The aria label for the dismiss button. | `string` |

Page tabs are for showing content for items like cases or entities and can be opened, closed and rearranged.

```tsx
import { DocumentIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { PageTab, PageTabGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/page-tabs';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const PageTabsExample: FC = () => {
  const [tabs, setTabs] = useState([
    {
      id: 'overview',
      label: 'Overview',
      content: 'Overview tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'features',
      label: 'Features',
      content: 'Features tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'installation',
      label: 'Installation',
      content: 'Installation tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    { id: 'usage', label: 'Usage', content: 'Usage tab', icon: <DocumentIcon /> },
    {
      id: 'support',
      label: 'Support',
      content: 'Support tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'resources',
      label: 'Resources',
      content: 'Resources tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    { id: 'faq', label: 'FAQ', content: 'FAQ tab', icon: <DocumentIcon /> },
    {
      id: 'contact',
      label: 'Contact',
      content: 'Contact tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'privacy',
      label: 'Privacy',
      content: 'Privacy tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    { id: 'terms', label: 'Terms', content: 'Terms tab', icon: <DocumentIcon /> },
  ]);

  const closeTab = (id: string): void => {
    setTabs(prevTabs => prevTabs.filter(tab => tab.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <PageTabGroup onTabClose={closeTab}>
      {tabs.map((tab, index) => (
        <PageTab key={index} id={tab.id} label={tab.label} icon={tab.icon}>
          <div style={{ padding: '1rem', height: 340 }}>{tab.content}</div>
        </PageTab>
      ))}
    </PageTabGroup>
  );
};

export default PageTabsExample;
```

## Import

Import `PageTabGroup` and `PageTab` components to use the Page Tabs.

```ts
import { PageTabGroup, PageTab } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/page-tabs';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/page-tabs`:

### PageTabGroup

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `selectedTab` | `string` | - | No | The id of the selected tab. |
| `onSelectedTabChange` | `string` | - | No | Event emitted when the selected state changes. |
| `size` | `small | medium` | - | No | The size of the page tabs. |
| `onTabClose` | `string` | - | No | Event emitted when a tab should be closed. The id of the tab that should be closed will be passed as an argument. |

### PageTab

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `id` | `string` | - | Yes | The unique id for this tab. |
| `label` | `string` | - | Yes | The label of the tab. |
| `icon` | `ReactElement` | - | No | The icon of the tab. |
| `isRemovable` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether this tab is removable. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Page Tabs component in different scenarios.

### Small tabs

```tsx
      label: 'Features',

      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'installation',
      label: 'Installation',
      content: 'Installation tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,

    { id: 'usage', label: 'Usage', content: 'Usage tab', icon: <DocumentIcon /> },
    {
      id: 'support',
      label: 'Support',
```

### Level Zero

Depending on whether the page tabs are placed directly on the page or within a container the styling of the page tabs can be inverted.

```tsx
import { DocumentIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { PageTab, PageTabGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/page-tabs';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const PageTabsLevelZeroExample: FC = () => {
  const [tabs, setTabs] = useState([
    {
      id: 'overview',
      label: 'Overview',
      content: 'Overview tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'features',
      label: 'Features',
      content: 'Features tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'installation',
      label: 'Installation',
      content: 'Installation tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    { id: 'usage', label: 'Usage', content: 'Usage tab', icon: <DocumentIcon /> },
    {
      id: 'support',
      label: 'Support',
      content: 'Support tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'resources',
      label: 'Resources',
      content: 'Resources tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    { id: 'faq', label: 'FAQ', content: 'FAQ tab', icon: <DocumentIcon /> },
    {
      id: 'contact',
      label: 'Contact',
      content: 'Contact tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    {
      id: 'privacy',
      label: 'Privacy',
      content: 'Privacy tab',
      icon: <DocumentIcon />,
    },
    { id: 'terms', label: 'Terms', content: 'Terms tab', icon: <DocumentIcon /> },
  ]);

  const closeTab = (id: string): void => {
    setTabs(prevTabs => prevTabs.filter(tab => tab.id !== id));
  };

  return (
    <PageTabGroup onTabClose={closeTab} level={0}>
      {tabs.map((tab, index) => (
        <PageTab key={index} id={tab.id} label={tab.label} icon={tab.icon}>
          <div style={{ padding: '1rem', height: 340 }}>{tab.content}</div>
        </PageTab>
      ))}
    </PageTabGroup>
  );
};

export default PageTabsLevelZeroExample;
```

---

A pagination control helps to navigate in a data table which contains a large number of items. It allows the user to jump to the next or previous pages of a result set. Contextual navigation can further help the user to find items in the list.

[UXD guidelines](https://uxd.opentext.net/guidelines/pagination.htm)

```tsx
import { Pagination } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/pagination';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const PaginationExample: FC = () => {
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);

  return <Pagination page={page} pageCount={10} limit={5} onPageChange={setPage} />;
};

export default PaginationExample;
```

## Import

Import `Pagination` to use the component.

```ts
import { Pagination } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/pagination';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/pagination`:

### Pagination

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `page` | `number` | `1` | No | The active page. |
| `pageCount` | `number` | `1` | No | The total number of pages. |
| `limit` | `number` | `5` | No | The maximum number of pages to display. |
| `onPageChange` | `(page: number) => void` | - | No | Event emitted when the active page changes. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the pagination component in different scenarios.

### Pagination Bar

The pagination component can be accompanied by a page size selector and items count to create a pagination bar.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { ChevronSmallDownIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { Menu, MenuItem, useMenuTrigger } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/menu';
import { Pagination, PaginationCount, PaginationBar } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/pagination';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const PaginationBarExample: FC = () => {
  const { menuProps, triggerProps } = useMenuTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>();
  const itemCount = 250;
  const [itemsPerPage, setItemsPerPage] = useState(25);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const pageCount = Math.ceil(itemCount / itemsPerPage);

  return (
    <>
      <PaginationBar
        dropdown={
          <Button
            flat
            secondary
            size="small"
            {...triggerProps}
            iconAfter={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />}
          >
            {itemsPerPage} per page
          </Button>
        }
        itemCount={<PaginationCount>{itemCount} items</PaginationCount>}
      >
        <Pagination page={page} pageCount={pageCount} onPageChange={setPage} />
      </PaginationBar>

      <Menu hasArrow={false} {...menuProps}>
        <MenuItem onClick={() => setItemsPerPage(25)}>25 per page</MenuItem>
        <MenuItem onClick={() => setItemsPerPage(50)}>50 per page</MenuItem>
        <MenuItem onClick={() => setItemsPerPage(100)}>100 per page</MenuItem>
      </Menu>
    </>
  );
};

export default PaginationBarExample;
```

### Pagination Bar End Alignment

The pagination controls can be aligned to the end of the pagination bar.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { ChevronSmallDownIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { Menu, MenuItem, useMenuTrigger } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/menu';
import { Pagination, PaginationCount, PaginationBar } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/pagination';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const PaginationBarAlignEndExample: FC = () => {
  const { menuProps, triggerProps } = useMenuTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>();
  const itemCount = 250;
  const [itemsPerPage, setItemsPerPage] = useState(25);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const pageCount = Math.ceil(itemCount / itemsPerPage);

  return (
    <>
      <PaginationBar
        alignment="end"
        dropdown={
          <Button
            flat
            secondary
            size="small"
            {...triggerProps}
            iconAfter={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />}
          >
            {itemsPerPage} per page
          </Button>
        }
        itemCount={<PaginationCount>{itemCount} items</PaginationCount>}
      >
        <Pagination page={page} pageCount={pageCount} onPageChange={setPage} />
      </PaginationBar>

      <Menu hasArrow={false} {...menuProps}>
        <MenuItem onClick={() => setItemsPerPage(25)}>25 per page</MenuItem>
        <MenuItem onClick={() => setItemsPerPage(50)}>50 per page</MenuItem>
        <MenuItem onClick={() => setItemsPerPage(100)}>100 per page</MenuItem>
      </Menu>
    </>
  );
};

export default PaginationBarAlignEndExample;
```

## Localization

This component uses several strings which must be localized. You can use `PaginationI18nContext` to configure these strings.

```tsx
import { PaginationI18nContext } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/pagination';

<PaginationI18nContext.Provider
  value={{
    previousButtonAriaLabel: 'Page précédente',
    nextButtonAriaLabel: 'Page suivante',
    firstButtonAriaLabel: 'Première page',
    lastButtonAriaLabel: 'Dernière page',
    getPageAriaLabel: (page: number) => `Page ${page}`,
  }}
>
  <Pagination />
</PaginationI18nContext.Provider>;
```

The following strings are available for localization:

| Name                        | Description                                  | Type                       |
| --------------------------- | -------------------------------------------- | -------------------------- |
| **previousButtonAriaLabel** | The aria label for the previous menu button. | `string`                   |
| **nextButtonAriaLabel**     | The aria label for the next menu button.     | `string`                   |
| **firstButtonAriaLabel**    | The aria label for the first menu button.    | `string`                   |
| **lastButtonAriaLabel**     | The aria label for the last menu button.     | `string`                   |
| **getPageAriaLabel**        | The aria label for a specific page.          | `(page: number) => string` |

---

This component is used for the type of search that is always available in a UI for users to find content.

```tsx
import { CloseSmallIcon, SearchSmallIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import {
  InputGroup,
  SearchButton,
  SearchClearButton,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/input-group';
import { TextInput } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const PersistentSearchExample: FC = () => {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');

  return (
    <InputGroup
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
      trailingAddon={
        <>
          <SearchClearButton icon={<CloseSmallIcon />} />
          <SearchButton icon={<SearchSmallIcon />} />
        </>
      }
    >
      <TextInput value={search} onValueChange={setSearch} placeholder="Search" type="search" />
    </InputGroup>
  );
};

export default PersistentSearchExample;
```

## Import

Import `TextInput` to use the component.

```ts
import { TextInput } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input`:

### TextInput

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `placeholder` | `string` | - | No | The placeholder text to display in the input field. |
| `value` | `string` | - | No | The value of the input field. |
| `onValueChange` | `(value: string) => void` | - | No | Event emits when the value of the input changes. |

### InputGroup

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Define the disabled state of the input group. |
| `size` | `'small' | 'medium' | 'large'` | - | No | Define the size of the input group. |
| `leadingAddon` | `ReactElement` | - | No | Define a leading addon. |
| `trailingAddon` | `ReactElement` | - | No | Define a trailing addon. |

### SearchButton

This is a thin wrapper around the Icon Button component. It automatically shows/hides the button based on the value of the input field and adapts its appearance based on the focused state of the input field.

### SearchClearButton

This is a thin wrapper around the Icon Button component. It automatically shows/hides the button based on the value and focused state of the input field.

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Persistent Search component in different scenarios.

### Small

The search field can have a small appearance.

```tsx

  InputGroup,
  SearchButton,
  SearchClearButton,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/input-group';
import { TextInput } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const PersistentSearchSmallExample: FC = () => {
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');

  return (
    <InputGroup
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
      size="small"
      trailingAddon={
        <>
          <SearchClearButton icon={<CloseSmallIcon />} />
          <SearchButton icon={<SearchSmallIcon />} />
        </>
      }
    >
      <TextInput value={search} onValueChange={setSearch} placeholder="Search" type="search" />
    </InputGroup>
  );
};

export default PersistentSearchSmallExample;
```

```ts
import { ProgressBar } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/progress-bar';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/progress-bar`:

### ProgressBar

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `value` | `number` | `0` | No | The value the bar should represent. This will be compared to the min/max value to determine the width of the bar. |
| `min` | `number` | `0` | No | The minimum allowed value for the control. |
| `max` | `number` | `100` | No | The maximum allowed value for the control. |

Radio buttons are used for choosing a single option out of a list.

```tsx
import { RadioButton, RadioGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/radio-button';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const RadioButtonExample: FC = () => {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('option-1');

  return (
    <RadioGroup value={value} onValueChange={setValue}>
      <RadioButton value="option-1">Option 1</RadioButton>
      <RadioButton value="option-2">Option 2</RadioButton>
      <RadioButton value="option-3">Option 3</RadioButton>
    </RadioGroup>
  );
};

export default RadioButtonExample;
```

## Import

Import `RadioButton` to use the component.

```ts
import { RadioButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/radio-button';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/radio-button`:

### RadioGroup

The radio group component is used to group radio buttons together.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `orientation` | `'horizontal' | 'vertical'` | - | No | The orientation of the radio buttons. |
| `value` | `string` | - | No | The currently selected option. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the radio group is disabled. |
| `isReadonly` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the radio group is read-only. |
| `onValueChange` | `(value: string) => void` | - | No | Function for handling updates to the selected option. |

### RadioButton

The radio button represents a single option in a group of radio buttons.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `value` | `string` | - | No | The value of this radio button. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the radio button should be disabled. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Radio Button component in different scenarios.

```tsx
import { RadioButton, RadioGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/radio-button';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const RadioButtonExample: FC = () => {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('option-1');

  return (
    <RadioGroup value={value} onValueChange={setValue}>
      <RadioButton value="option-1">Option 1</RadioButton>
      <RadioButton value="option-2">Option 2</RadioButton>
      <RadioButton value="option-3">Option 3</RadioButton>
    </RadioGroup>
  );
};

export default RadioButtonExample;
```

### Orientation

The orientation of the radio buttons can be set to either `horizontal` or `vertical`.

```tsx
import { RadioButton, RadioGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/radio-button';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const RadioButtonHorizontalExample: FC = () => {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('option-1');

  return (
    <RadioGroup orientation="horizontal" value={value} onValueChange={setValue}>
      <RadioButton value="option-1">Option 1</RadioButton>
      <RadioButton value="option-2">Option 2</RadioButton>
      <RadioButton value="option-3">Option 3</RadioButton>
    </RadioGroup>
  );
};

export default RadioButtonHorizontalExample;
```

### Disabled

Radio buttons can be disabled by setting the `isDisabled` property to `true`.

```tsx
import { RadioButton, RadioGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/radio-button';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const RadioButtonDisabledExample: FC = () => {
  const [value, setValue] = useState('option-1');

  return (
    <RadioGroup isDisabled value={value} onValueChange={setValue}>
      <RadioButton value="option-1">Option 1</RadioButton>
      <RadioButton value="option-2">Option 2</RadioButton>
      <RadioButton value="option-3">Option 3</RadioButton>
    </RadioGroup>
  );
};

export default RadioButtonDisabledExample;
```

---

The select input allows users to make a selection from a pre-populated list of options.

```tsx
import { Select, SelectOption, SelectProps } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const SelectExample: FC<SelectProps<string>> = () => {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>();

  return (
    <Select<string>
      value={value}
      onValueChange={setValue}
      placeholder="Select an option"
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
    >
      <SelectOption value="option-1">Option 1</SelectOption>
      <SelectOption value="option-2">Option 2</SelectOption>
      <SelectOption value="option-3">Option 3</SelectOption>
    </Select>
  );
};

export default SelectExample;
```

## Import

Import `Select` to use the component.

```ts
import { Select } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/select`:

### Select

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `value` | `T` | - | - | Value of the control. |
| `size` | `small | medium | large` | `medium` | - | The size of the Select component. |
| `placeholder` | `string | ReactNode` | - | - | Text to show when no value is selected. |
| `dropdownPlacement` | `auto | top | bottom` | `auto` | - | Where the dropdown should be aligned relative to the control. By default, the dropdown will open below if there is enough space, otherwise it will open above. |
| `isOpen` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether the dropdown is visible. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(isOpen: boolean) => void` | - | - | Emitted when the user opens or closes the options panel. |
| `valueRender` | `(value: T) => ReactNode` | - | - | Define a custom renderer for the selected value. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether the control is disabled. |
| `onValueChange` | `(value: T) => void` | - | - | Emitted when the user modifies the value. |
| `maxHeight` | `string | number` | - | - | The max height of the option panel. Accepts a number of pixels, or any CSS value with units. |
| `clearable` | `boolean` | - | - | Whether the select should show a clear button. |
| `ariaLabel` | `string` | - | - | Aria label for the component. |
| `ariaLabelledBy` | `string` | - | - | Aria labelled by for the component. |
| `displayWith` | `(value: T) => string` | - | - | Function used to transform values to display strings. |
| `defaultValue` | `T` | - | - | The initial value of the component. |
| `panelWidth` | `string | number` | - | - | The width of the dropdown panel. |
| `onScrolledToEnd` | `() => void` | - | - | Emitted when the user scrolls to the end of the list. |
| `container` | `HTMLElement` | - | No | The container element the select dropdown will be appended to. This defaults to the document body. |

## Examples

### Sizes

The select component supports three sizes: small, medium, and large.

```tsx
import { Select, SelectOption, SelectProps } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const SelectSizesExample: FC<SelectProps<string>> = () => {
  const [valueSmall, setValueSmall] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const [valueMedium, setValueMedium] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const [valueLarge, setValueLarge] = useState<string | undefined>(undefined);

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        width: '300px',
        gap: 16,
      }}
    >
      <Select<string>
        value={valueSmall}
        onValueChange={setValueSmall}
        size="small"
        placeholder="Small size variant"
      >
        <SelectOption value="option-1">Option 1</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-2">Option 2</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-3">Option 3</SelectOption>
      </Select>

      <Select<string>
        value={valueMedium}
        onValueChange={setValueMedium}
        size="medium"
        placeholder="Medium size variant"
      >
        <SelectOption value="option-1">Option 1</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-2">Option 2</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-3">Option 3</SelectOption>
      </Select>

      <Select<string>
        value={valueLarge}
        onValueChange={setValueLarge}
        size="large"
        placeholder="Large size variant"
      >
        <SelectOption value="option-1">Option 1</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-2">Option 2</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-3">Option 3</SelectOption>
      </Select>
    </div>
  );
};

export default SelectSizesExample;
```

### Clearable

The select component has the option to add a clear button.

```tsx
import { Select, SelectOption } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const SelectClearableExample: FC = () => {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>('option-1');

  return (
    <Select<string>
      value={value}
      clearable={true}
      onValueChange={setValue}
      placeholder="Select an option"
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
    >
      <SelectOption value="option-1">Option 1</SelectOption>
      <SelectOption value="option-2">Option 2</SelectOption>
      <SelectOption value="option-3">Option 3</SelectOption>
    </Select>
  );
};

export default SelectClearableExample;
```

### Option Group Headings

The select component supports option group headings.

```tsx
import {
  Select,
  SelectProps,
  SelectOption,
  SelectGroup,
  SelectGroupHeader,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const SelectGroupExample: FC<SelectProps<string>> = () => {
  const [value, setValue] = useState<string | undefined>();

  return (
    <Select<string>
      value={value}
      onValueChange={setValue}
      placeholder="Select an option"
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
    >
      <SelectGroup>
        <SelectGroupHeader>Group 1</SelectGroupHeader>
        <SelectOption value="option-1">Option 1</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-2">Option 2</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-3">Option 3</SelectOption>
      </SelectGroup>

      <SelectGroup>
        <SelectGroupHeader>Group 2</SelectGroupHeader>
        <SelectOption value="option-4">Option 4</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-5">Option 5</SelectOption>
        <SelectOption value="option-6">Option 6</SelectOption>
      </SelectGroup>
    </Select>
  );
};

export default SelectGroupExample;
```

### Load more on scroll

The select component supports loading more options when the user scrolls to the bottom of the list using the `onScrolledToEnd` event.

```tsx
import { Select, SelectOption } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/select';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const SelectLoadMoreExample: FC = () => {
  const [options, setOptions] = useState(Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => `Option ${i + 1}`));
  const [currentPage, setCurrentPage] = useState(1);

  const loadNextPage = async (): Promise<void> => {
    // simulate a delay of 1/2 second
    await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 500));

    const nextPage = Array.from({ length: 20 }, (_, i) => `Option ${currentPage * 20 + i + 1}`);
    setOptions([...options, ...nextPage]);
    setCurrentPage(currentPage + 1);
  };

  return (
    <Select
      placeholder="Select an option"
      onScrolledToEnd={loadNextPage}
      style={{ width: '300px' }}
    >
      {options.map(option => (
        <SelectOption key={option} value={option}>
          {option}
        </SelectOption>
      ))}
    </Select>
  );
};

export default SelectLoadMoreExample;
```

---

Sign in provides a user-friendly interface for authentication, featuring customizable fields and responsive design.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { Form, FormActionGroup, FormField } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/form';
import { ChevronSmallDownIcon, LanguageIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import {
  LoginLink,
  LoginPage,
  LoginPanel,
  LoginTitle,
  LoginTitleSecondary,
  LoginVersion,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/login';
import { OpentextLoginLogo } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/logo';
import { Menu, MenuItem, useMenuTrigger } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/menu';
import { TextInput } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/text-input';
import { Link } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/typography';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const SignInExample: FC = () => {
  const languages: LanguageOption[] = [
    { language: 'en-US', name: 'English' },
    { language: 'es-ES', name: 'Español' },
    { language: 'it-IT', name: 'Italiano' },
    { language: 'tr-TR', name: 'Türkçe' },
  ];

  const [selectedLanguage, setSelectedLanguage] = useState<LanguageOption>(languages[0]);

  const { menuProps, triggerProps } = useMenuTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>();
  const [username, setUsername] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  return (
    <>
      <LoginPage
        width="100%"
        height="100%"
        leadingFooter={
          <p className="copyright">
            © 2026 Open Text. Patents may cover this product, see{' '}
            <Link target="_blank" href="javascript:void(0)">
              https://www.opentext.com/patents
            </Link>
            .
          </p>
        }
        trailingFooter={
          <>
            <Button
              flat
              secondary
              size="small"
              iconBefore={<LanguageIcon />}
              iconAfter={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />}
              {...triggerProps}
            >
              {selectedLanguage.name}
            </Button>
            <LoginLink href="javascript:void(0)">Privacy policy</LoginLink>
            <LoginLink href="javascript:void(0)">Terms</LoginLink>
            <LoginLink href="javascript:void(0)">Help & Support</LoginLink>
          </>
        }
      >
        <LoginPanel
          productTitle={
            <LoginTitle>
              Primary Name
              <LoginTitleSecondary>Secondary name</LoginTitleSecondary>
              <LoginVersion>25.2</LoginVersion>
            </LoginTitle>
          }
          brandImage={<OpentextLoginLogo />}
        >
          <Form>
            <FormField>
              <TextInput
                id="username"
                placeholder="Username"
                size="large"
                value={username}
                onValueChange={setUsername}
              />
            </FormField>

            <FormField>
              <TextInput
                id="password"
                type="password"
                placeholder="Password"
                size="large"
                value={password}
                onValueChange={setPassword}
              />
            </FormField>

            <div className="demo-remember-container">
              <Checkbox>Remember me</Checkbox>
              <Link href="javascript:void(0)">Forgot password?</Link>
            </div>

            <FormActionGroup>
              <Button type="submit" size="large" primary isDisabled={!(username && password)}>
                Sign in
              </Button>
            </FormActionGroup>
          </Form>
        </LoginPanel>
      </LoginPage>
      <Menu hasArrow={false} {...menuProps}>
        {languages.map(language => (
          <MenuItem key={language.language} onClick={() => setSelectedLanguage(language)}>
            {language.name}
          </MenuItem>
        ))}
      </Menu>

      <style>
        {`
      /** Remove padding from the body */
      body.sb-show-main.sb-main-padded {
        padding: 0;
      }

      .demo-remember-container {
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        justify-content: space-between;

        a {
          font-size: 12px;
        }
      }

      .copyright {
        margin: 0;
      }

      /** When the login component is 320px or smaller we stack the remember me and forgot password links */
      @container login (max-width: 320px) {
        .demo-remember-container {
          flex-direction: column;
          row-gap: 40px;
          align-items: flex-start;

          a {
            display: block;
            width: 100%;
            text-align: center;
          }
        }
      }
    `}
      </style>
    </>
  );
};

interface LanguageOption {
  language: string;
  name: string;
}

export default SignInExample;
```

```ts
  LoginLink,
  LoginPageBackdrop,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/login';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/login`:

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `width` | `string` | `100vw` | No | Width of the container as a CSS value. Default: 100vw. |
| `height` | `string` | `100vh` | No | Height of the container as a CSS value. Default: 100vh. |
| `backdrop` | `ReactNode` | `` | No | The backdrop to display behind the sign in panel. |
| `leadingFooter` | `ReactNode` | - | No | The leading footer content. |
| `trailingFooter` | `ReactNode` | - | No | The trailing footer content. |

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `icon` | `ReactNode` | - | No | The product icon. |
| `productTitle` | `ReactNode` | - | No | The product title. |
| `brandImage` | `ReactNode` | - | No | The brand logo. |

### LoginTitle

The product name.

### LoginTitleSecondary

The secondary product name.

### LoginVersion

The product version.

### LoginLink

Identify links to other pages or resources.

## Content Security Policy

The sign in components uses `data:` URIs to display the various background images for the different screen sizes. However if you have strict Content Security Policy (CSP) rules in place,
this will prevent the images from being displayed. To allow the sign in components to work with CSP, you will need to download and add the following images to the assets folder in your application:

- Download medium background
- Download large background
- Download extra large background

Then add the following to your global stylesheet, updating the paths to point to the correct location:

```css
.uxa-theme-jato-light,
.uxa-theme-jato-dark {
  --uxa-login-page-backdrop__background--md: url('/public/signin-md.svg');
  --uxa-login-page-backdrop__background--lg: url('/public/signin-lg.svg');
  --uxa-login-page-backdrop__background--xl: url('/public/signin-xl.svg');
}
```

---

A skip link is an accessibility feature that allows users to tab directly to the main content of a page.

```tsx
import { SkipLink, useSkipLinkTarget } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/a11y';
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { MenuIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { OpentextSmallLogo } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/logo';
import {
  Masthead,
  MastheadTitle,
  MastheadTitleSecondary,
  MastheadVersion,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/masthead';
import { Panel } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/panel';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef } from 'react';

const SkipLinkExample: FC = () => {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const skipLinkProps = useSkipLinkTarget(ref);

  return (
    <>
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', flexDirection: 'column', rowGap: 16 }}>
        <SkipLink>Skip to main content</SkipLink>

        <Masthead
          logo={<OpentextSmallLogo />}
          menuButton={<IconButton flat primary icon={<MenuIcon />} aria-label="menu button" />}
          title={
            <MastheadTitle>
              Primary name
              <MastheadTitleSecondary>Secondary name</MastheadTitleSecondary>
              <MastheadVersion>24.1</MastheadVersion>
            </MastheadTitle>
          }
        />

        <Panel ref={ref} {...skipLinkProps}>
          This panel contains the main content and will be focused when the skip link is activated.
          Tab on to the page to show the skip link.
        </Panel>
      </div>

      <style>
        {`
      /** Remove padding from the body */
      body.sb-show-main.sb-main-padded {
        padding: 0;
      }

      .uxa-panel {
        margin-inline: 16px;
      }
        `}
      </style>
    </>
  );
};

export default SkipLinkExample;
```

Skip links are hidden by default and are only visible when focused. They are typically the first element in the tab order and are used to skip over navigation menus and other repetitive content.

## Usage

The skip link component is an anchor element that can be used to navigate to a specific target on the page. The target can be specified either by using the `useSkipLinkTarget` hook or by providing a `href` attribute.

If there is no target element and no `href` attribute, the skip link will not be focusable.

The skip link component should be located before the main content of the page and so that it is the first focusable element on the page.

### Skip Link Target

To create a target for a skip link use the `useSkipLinkTarget` hook. This is the preferred approach as this dynamically updated the target element as routes change or element visibility changes.

```tsx
import { useSkipLinkTarget } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/a11y';
import { useRef } from 'react';

function App() {
  const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const { targetProps } = useSkipLinkTarget(ref);

  return (
    <>
      <SkipLink>Skip to main content</SkipLink>;
      <div ref={ref} {...targetProps}>
        Main content
      </div>
    </>
  );
}
```

### Skip Link Href

To navigate to a specific target, provide a `href` attribute to the skip link. This href must match the `id` of the target element and the target element must have a `tabindex` attribute set. If the target element should not be focusable by default, set the `tabindex` attribute to `-1`.

```tsx
<SkipLink href="#main-content">Skip to main content</SkipLink>;

<div id="main-content" tabIndex={-1}>
  Main content
</div>;
```

## Import

Import the `SkipLink` to use the skip link component.

```ts
import { SkipLink, useSkipLinkTarget } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/a11y';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/a11y`:

### SkipLink

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the skip link is disabled. |
| `href` | `string` | - | No | The href to navigate to. |

### useSkipLinkTarget

A hook that creates a target for a skip link given a reference to an element and returns the props to apply to the target element.

---

Split or segmented buttons are a good way to group actions of the same type, to save space, reduce cognitive load, or separate primary from secondary actions. It is important to use this option only if the actions in the drop down are variations of the same action. The split button should not be used just to group actions that are somehow loosely related, or merely similar in what they do.

[UXD guidelines](https://uxd.opentext.net/guidelines/buttons_interaction.htm)

```tsx
import { Button, IconButton, SplitButtonGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { ChevronSmallDownIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const SplitButtonExample: FC = () => (
  <SplitButtonGroup variant="primary">
    <Button>Button</Button>
    <IconButton icon={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />} aria-label="dropdown button" />
  </SplitButtonGroup>
);

export default SplitButtonExample;
```

## Import

Import `SplitButtonGroup` to use the component.

```ts
import { SplitButtonGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/button`:

### SplitButtonGroup

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `variant` | `'primary' | 'secondary'` | - | Yes | The color variant of the buttons within the group. |
| `size` | `'small' | 'medium'` | `medium` | No | The size of the buttons within the group. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the buttons should be disabled. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Split Button component in different scenarios.

### Secondary Split Button

The split button can be presented in a secondary color variant.

```tsx
import { Button, IconButton, SplitButtonGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { ChevronSmallDownIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const SplitButtonSecondaryExample: FC = () => (
  <SplitButtonGroup variant="secondary">
    <Button>Button</Button>
    <IconButton icon={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />} aria-label="dropdown button" />
  </SplitButtonGroup>
);

export default SplitButtonSecondaryExample;
```

### Small Split Button

The split button can be presented in a smaller size.

```tsx
import { Button, IconButton, SplitButtonGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { ChevronSmallDownIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const SplitButtonSmallExample: FC = () => (
  <SplitButtonGroup variant="primary" size="small">
    <Button>Button</Button>
    <IconButton icon={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />} aria-label="dropdown button" />
  </SplitButtonGroup>
);

export default SplitButtonSmallExample;
```

### Large Split Button

The split button can be presented in a larger size.

