```tsx
import { FC } from 'react';

const SplitButtonLargeExample: FC = () => (
  <SplitButtonGroup variant="primary" size="large">
    <Button>Button</Button>
    <IconButton icon={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />} aria-label="dropdown button" />
  </SplitButtonGroup>
);

export default SplitButtonLargeExample;
```

---

The splitter is a flexible layout component that lets you divide your interface into resizable, collapsible sections. Drag to adjust sizes and create dynamic, customizable layouts.

```tsx
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/card';
import {
  Splitter,
  SplitterArea,
  SplitterAreaProps,
  SplitterSize,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/splitter';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const AreaData: FC<SplitterAreaProps & { title: string }> = ({ title, size, children }) => (
  <div style={{ height: '100%', padding: '1px' }}>
    <Card style={{ height: '100%', overflow: 'auto' }}>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent size="small">
        size: {typeof size === 'number' ? size.toFixed(1) : size ?? 'auto'}
        <br />
        {children}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  </div>
);

const SplitterHorizontalExample: FC = () => {
  const [area1Size, setArea1Size] = useState<SplitterSize>();
  const [area2Size, setArea2Size] = useState<SplitterSize>();
  const [area2aSize, setArea2aSize] = useState<SplitterSize>();
  const [area2bSize, setArea2bSize] = useState<SplitterSize>();

  return (
    <Splitter orientation="horizontal">
      <SplitterArea
        key="area-1"
        size={area1Size}
        onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea1Size(size)}
      >
        <AreaData title="Area 1" size={area1Size}>
          isResizable: true
        </AreaData>
      </SplitterArea>
      <SplitterArea
        key="area-2"
        size={area2Size}
        onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea2Size(size)}
      >
        <Splitter orientation="vertical">
          <SplitterArea
            key="area-2a"
            size={area2aSize}
            onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea2aSize(size)}
          >
            <AreaData title="Area 2A" size={area2aSize}>
              isResizable: true
            </AreaData>
          </SplitterArea>
          <SplitterArea
            key="area-2b"
            size={area2bSize}
            onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea2bSize(size)}
          >
            <AreaData title="Area 2B" size={area2bSize}>
              isResizable: true
            </AreaData>
          </SplitterArea>
        </Splitter>
      </SplitterArea>
    </Splitter>
  );
};

export default SplitterHorizontalExample;
```

## Import

Import `Splitter` to use the component.

```ts
import { Splitter } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/splitter';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/splitter`:

### Splitter

- Selector: `Splitter`

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `orientation` | `horizontal | vertical` | `horizontal` | No | The orientation of the splitter container. |
| `handleAriaLabel` | `string` | - | No | Aria label for the resize drag handle. |

### SplitterArea

- Selector: `SplitterArea`

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isResizable` | `boolean` | `true` | - | Whether the splitter area can be resized. |
| `isCollapsed` | `boolean` | `false` | - | Whether the splitter area is collapsed. |
| `size` | `number | 'auto'` | - | - | The initial size of the splitter area, or the fixed size if the splitter area is not resizable. |
| `defaultSize` | `number | 'auto'` | - | - | The default size of the splitter area when used in an uncontrolled mode. |
| `minSize` | `number` | `0` | - | The minimum size of the splitter area. |
| `maxSize` | `number` | - | - | The maximum size of the splitter area. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the splitter in different scenarios.

### Horizontal splitter

By default, the splitter orientation is set to `'horizontal'`.

```tsx
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/card';
import {
  Splitter,
  SplitterArea,
  SplitterAreaProps,
  SplitterSize,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/splitter';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const AreaData: FC<SplitterAreaProps & { title: string }> = ({ title, size, children }) => (
  <div style={{ height: '100%', padding: '1px' }}>
    <Card style={{ height: '100%', overflow: 'auto' }}>
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent size="small">
        size: {typeof size === 'number' ? size.toFixed(1) : size ?? 'auto'}
        <br />
        {children}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  </div>
);

const SplitterHorizontalExample: FC = () => {
  const [area1Size, setArea1Size] = useState<SplitterSize>();
  const [area2Size, setArea2Size] = useState<SplitterSize>(150);

  return (
    <Splitter orientation="horizontal">
      <SplitterArea
        key="area-1"
        size={area1Size}
        onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea1Size(size)}
      >
        <AreaData title="Area 1" size={area1Size}>
          isResizable: true
        </AreaData>
      </SplitterArea>
      <SplitterArea
        key="area-2"
        minSize={100}
        maxSize={200}
        size={area2Size}
        onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea2Size(size)}
      >
        <AreaData title="Area 2" size={area2Size}>
          minSize: 100
          <br />
          maxSize: 200
          <br />
          isResizable: true
        </AreaData>
      </SplitterArea>
      <SplitterArea key="area-3" size={120} isResizable={false}>
        <AreaData title="Area 3" size={120}>
          isResizable: false
        </AreaData>
      </SplitterArea>
    </Splitter>
  );
};

export default SplitterHorizontalExample;
```

### Vertical splitter

The splitter can be displayed vertically by setting the `orientation` input to `'vertical'`.

```tsx
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/card';
import {
  Splitter,
  SplitterArea,
  SplitterAreaProps,
  SplitterSize,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/splitter';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const AreaData: FC<SplitterAreaProps & { title: string }> = ({ title, size, children }) => (
  <div style={{ height: '100%', padding: '1px' }}>
    <Card style={{ height: '100%', overflow: 'auto' }}>
      {' '}
      <CardHeader>
        <CardTitle>{title}</CardTitle>
      </CardHeader>
      <CardContent size="small">
        size: {typeof size === 'number' ? size.toFixed(1) : size ?? 'auto'}
        <br />
        {children}
      </CardContent>
    </Card>
  </div>
);

const SplitterVerticalExample: FC = () => {
  const [area1Size, setArea1Size] = useState<SplitterSize>('auto');
  const [area2Size, setArea2Size] = useState<SplitterSize>(160);

  return (
    <Splitter orientation="vertical">
      <SplitterArea
        key="area-1"
        size={area1Size}
        onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea1Size(size)}
      >
        <AreaData title="Area 1" size={area1Size}>
          isResizable: true
        </AreaData>
      </SplitterArea>
      <SplitterArea
        key="area-2"
        minSize={100}
        maxSize={200}
        size={area2Size}
        onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea2Size(size)}
      >
        <AreaData title="Area 2" size={area2Size}>
          minSize: 100
          <br />
          maxSize: 200
          <br />
          isResizable: true
        </AreaData>
      </SplitterArea>
      <SplitterArea key="area-3" size={120} isResizable={false}>
        <AreaData title="Area 3" size={120}>
          isResizable: false
        </AreaData>
      </SplitterArea>
    </Splitter>
  );
};

export default SplitterVerticalExample;
```

### Collapsible splitter areas

Splitter areas can contain collapsed and expanded states by setting the `isCollapsed` input.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/card';
import { Splitter, SplitterArea, SplitterSize } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/splitter';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const SplitterCollapsibleExample: FC = () => {
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [area1Size, setArea1Size] = useState<SplitterSize>();
  const [area2Size, setArea2Size] = useState<SplitterSize>(150);
  return (
    <Splitter>
      <SplitterArea
        key="area-1"
        size={area1Size}
        onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea1Size(size)}
      >
        <div style={{ height: '100%', padding: '1px' }}>
          <Card
            style={{
              height: '100%',
              justifyContent: 'center',
              alignItems: 'center',
              overflow: 'auto',
            }}
          >
            <CardContent>
              <Button primary onClick={() => setCollapsed(!collapsed)}>
                {collapsed ? 'Expand' : 'Collapse'} Area
              </Button>
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </SplitterArea>

      <SplitterArea
        key="area-2"
        size={area2Size}
        isCollapsed={collapsed}
        onSizeChange={(size: SplitterSize) => setArea2Size(size)}
      >
        <div style={{ height: '100%', padding: '1px' }}>
          <Card style={{ height: '100%', overflow: 'auto' }}>
            <CardHeader>
              <CardTitle>Collapsible</CardTitle>
            </CardHeader>
            <CardContent size="small">
              size: {typeof area2Size === 'number' ? area2Size.toFixed(1) : area2Size ?? 'auto'}
              <br />
              collapsed: {collapsed.toString()}
              <br />
              resizable: true
            </CardContent>
          </Card>
        </div>
      </SplitterArea>
    </Splitter>
  );
};

export default SplitterCollapsibleExample;
```

## Localization

This component uses strings which should be localized. You can use `SplitterI18nContext` to configure these strings.

```tsx
import { provideSplitterI18n, UxaDefaultSplitterI18n } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/splitter';
import { SplitterI18nContext } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/splitter';

<SplitterI18nContext.Provider
  value={{
    handleAriaLabel: 'Poignée de déplacement du séparateur',
  }}
>
  <Splitter />
</SplitterI18nContext.Provider>;
```

The following strings are available for localization:

| Name                | Description                         | Type     |
| ------------------- | ----------------------------------- | -------- |
| **handleAriaLabel** | The aria label for the drag handle. | `string` |

---

A data table is a complex list which can display data, objects and associated attributes or metadata within columns and rows.

```tsx
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { DocumentIcon, StarIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { FolderIcon, PdfIcon, PodcastIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato-mime';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableHeaderCellSortDirection,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const resources: Resource[] = Array.from({ length: 16 }).map(() => ({
  name:
    faker.system.fileName({ extensionCount: 0 }) + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['.pdf', '.mp3', '']),
  size:
    faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 600 }) + ' ' + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['KB', 'MB', 'GB']),
  owner: faker.person.fullName(),
  status: faker.datatype.boolean(),
}));

const TableExample: FC = () => {
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<TableHeaderCellSortDirection>();
  const { selection, toggle, clear, isSelected, select } = useSelection<Resource>({
    mode: 'multiple',
  });

  const getIcon = (name: string): React.ReactNode => {
    switch (name.split('.').pop()) {
      case 'pdf':
        return <PdfIcon size="24px" />;
      case 'mp3':
        return <PodcastIcon size="24px" />;
      default:
        return <FolderIcon size="24px" />;
    }
  };

  const toggleSelectAll = (checked: boolean): void => {
    if (checked) {
      select(...resources);
    } else {
      clear();
    }
  };

  const sortedResources = useMemo(() => {
    if (sortDirection === 'ascending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    }

    if (sortDirection === 'descending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => b.name.localeCompare(a.name));
    }

    return resources;
  }, [sortDirection]);

  return (
    <Table style={{ width: '100%' }}>
      <TableHead>
        <TableRow>
          <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, minWidth: 40 }}>
            <Checkbox
              isChecked={selection.length === resources.length}
              isIndeterminate={selection.length > 0 && selection.length < resources.length}
              onCheckedChange={toggleSelectAll}
            />
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, lineHeight: '8px' }}>
            <DocumentIcon size="16px" />
          </TableHeaderCell>

          <TableHeaderCell
            isSortable
            sortDirection={sortDirection}
            onSortDirectionChange={setSortDirection}
          >
            Name
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell align="end" style={{ width: 68 }}>
            File size
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell>Owned by</TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell />
        </TableRow>
      </TableHead>
      <TableBody>
        {sortedResources.map(resource => (
          <TableRow
            key={resource.name}
            tabIndex={0}
            isSelected={isSelected(resource)}
            onClick={() => toggle(resource)}
            onKeyDown={e => {
              if (e.key === ' ') {
                toggle(resource);
              }
            }}
          >
            <TableCell align="center">
              <Checkbox inert isChecked={isSelected(resource)} />
            </TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>{getIcon(resource.name)}</TableCell>

            <TableCell>{resource.name}</TableCell>
            <TableCell align="end">{resource.size}</TableCell>
            <TableCell>{resource.owner}</TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>
              {resource.status && <StarIcon size="16px" />}
            </TableCell>
          </TableRow>
        ))}
      </TableBody>
    </Table>
  );
};

interface Resource {
  name: string;
  size: string;
  owner: string;
  status: boolean;
}

export default TableExample;
```

## Import

Import `Table` to use the component.

```ts
import {
  Table,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableBody,
  TableRow,
  TableCell,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
```

## API reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/table`:

### Table

The `Table` component is the main component for displaying tabular data.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `size` | `'small' | 'medium' | 'large'` | `medium` | No | Define the size of the table rows. |
| `isEmbedded` | `boolean` | - | No | Define if the table should use the embedded appearance. |
| `isStriped` | `boolean` | - | No | Define if the table should have striped rows. |

### TableHead

The `TableHead` component contains the the column headers for the table.

### TableHeaderCell

The `TableHeaderCell` component represents a column header within the table.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `align` | `'center' | 'end' | 'start'` | - | No | Define the horizontal alignment of the column header. |
| `isSortable` | `boolean` | - | No | Define if the column is sortable. |
| `sortDirection` | `ascending | descending | undefined` | - | No | Define the sort direction of the column. |
| `onSortDirectionChange` | `(direction: TableHeaderCellSortDirection) => void` | - | No | Event emitted when the sort direction of the column changes. |

### TableBody

The `TableBody` component contains the rows of the table.

### TableRow

The `TableRow` component represents a row within the table.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isSelected` | `boolean` | - | No | Define if the row is selected. |

### TableCell

The `TableCell` component represents a cell within a row of the table.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `align` | `'center' | 'end' | 'start'` | - | No | Define the horizontal alignment of the cell. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the table component in different scenarios.

### Embedded table

An embedded table is an alternate presentation for cases where a table is embedded within another component, such as a card or panel.

```tsx
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { DocumentIcon, StarIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { FolderIcon, PdfIcon, PodcastIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato-mime';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableHeaderCellSortDirection,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const resources: Resource[] = Array.from({ length: 16 }).map(() => ({
  name:
    faker.system.fileName({ extensionCount: 0 }) + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['.pdf', '.mp3', '']),
  size:
    faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 600 }) + ' ' + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['KB', 'MB', 'GB']),
  owner: faker.person.fullName(),
  status: faker.datatype.boolean(),
}));

const TableEmbeddedExample: FC = () => {
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<TableHeaderCellSortDirection>();
  const { selection, toggle, clear, isSelected, select } = useSelection<Resource>({
    mode: 'multiple',
  });

  const getIcon = (name: string): React.ReactNode => {
    switch (name.split('.').pop()) {
      case 'pdf':
        return <PdfIcon size="24px" />;
      case 'mp3':
        return <PodcastIcon size="24px" />;
      default:
        return <FolderIcon size="24px" />;
    }
  };

  const toggleSelectAll = (checked: boolean): void => {
    if (checked) {
      select(...resources);
    } else {
      clear();
    }
  };

  const sortedResources = useMemo(() => {
    if (sortDirection === 'ascending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    }

    if (sortDirection === 'descending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => b.name.localeCompare(a.name));
    }

    return resources;
  }, [sortDirection]);

  return (
    <Table style={{ width: '100%' }} isEmbedded>
      <TableHead>
        <TableRow>
          <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, minWidth: 40 }}>
            <Checkbox
              isChecked={selection.length === resources.length}
              isIndeterminate={selection.length > 0 && selection.length < resources.length}
              onCheckedChange={toggleSelectAll}
            />
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, lineHeight: '8px' }}>
            <DocumentIcon size="16px" />
          </TableHeaderCell>

          <TableHeaderCell
            isSortable
            sortDirection={sortDirection}
            onSortDirectionChange={setSortDirection}
          >
            Name
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell align="end" style={{ width: 68 }}>
            File size
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell>Owned by</TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell />
        </TableRow>
      </TableHead>
      <TableBody>
        {sortedResources.map(resource => (
          <TableRow
            key={resource.name}
            tabIndex={0}
            isSelected={isSelected(resource)}
            onClick={() => toggle(resource)}
            onKeyDown={e => {
              if (e.key === ' ') {
                toggle(resource);
              }
            }}
          >
            <TableCell align="center">
              <Checkbox inert isChecked={isSelected(resource)} />
            </TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>{getIcon(resource.name)}</TableCell>

            <TableCell>{resource.name}</TableCell>
            <TableCell align="end">{resource.size}</TableCell>
            <TableCell>{resource.owner}</TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>
              {resource.status && <StarIcon size="16px" />}
            </TableCell>
          </TableRow>
        ))}
      </TableBody>
    </Table>
  );
};

interface Resource {
  name: string;
  size: string;
  owner: string;
  status: boolean;
}

export default TableEmbeddedExample;
```

### Table with striped rows

A table can have striped rows where every other row is shaded.

```tsx
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { DocumentIcon, StarIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { FolderIcon, PdfIcon, PodcastIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato-mime';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableHeaderCellSortDirection,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const resources: Resource[] = Array.from({ length: 16 }).map(() => ({
  name:
    faker.system.fileName({ extensionCount: 0 }) + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['.pdf', '.mp3', '']),
  size:
    faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 600 }) + ' ' + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['KB', 'MB', 'GB']),
  owner: faker.person.fullName(),
  status: faker.datatype.boolean(),
}));

const TableStripedExample: FC = () => {
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<TableHeaderCellSortDirection>();
  const { selection, toggle, clear, isSelected, select } = useSelection<Resource>({
    mode: 'multiple',
  });

  const getIcon = (name: string): React.ReactNode => {
    switch (name.split('.').pop()) {
      case 'pdf':
        return <PdfIcon size="24px" />;
      case 'mp3':
        return <PodcastIcon size="24px" />;
      default:
        return <FolderIcon size="24px" />;
    }
  };

  const toggleSelectAll = (checked: boolean): void => {
    if (checked) {
      select(...resources);
    } else {
      clear();
    }
  };

  const sortedResources = useMemo(() => {
    if (sortDirection === 'ascending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    }

    if (sortDirection === 'descending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => b.name.localeCompare(a.name));
    }

    return resources;
  }, [sortDirection]);

  return (
    <Table style={{ width: '100%' }} isStriped>
      <TableHead>
        <TableRow>
          <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, minWidth: 40 }}>
            <Checkbox
              isChecked={selection.length === resources.length}
              isIndeterminate={selection.length > 0 && selection.length < resources.length}
              onCheckedChange={toggleSelectAll}
            />
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, lineHeight: '8px' }}>
            <DocumentIcon size="16px" />
          </TableHeaderCell>

          <TableHeaderCell
            isSortable
            sortDirection={sortDirection}
            onSortDirectionChange={setSortDirection}
          >
            Name
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell align="end" style={{ width: 68 }}>
            File size
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell>Owned by</TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell />
        </TableRow>
      </TableHead>
      <TableBody>
        {sortedResources.map(resource => (
          <TableRow
            key={resource.name}
            tabIndex={0}
            isSelected={isSelected(resource)}
            onClick={() => toggle(resource)}
            onKeyDown={e => {
              if (e.key === ' ') {
                toggle(resource);
              }
            }}
          >
            <TableCell align="center">
              <Checkbox inert isChecked={isSelected(resource)} />
            </TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>{getIcon(resource.name)}</TableCell>

            <TableCell>{resource.name}</TableCell>
            <TableCell align="end">{resource.size}</TableCell>
            <TableCell>{resource.owner}</TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>
              {resource.status && <StarIcon size="16px" />}
            </TableCell>
          </TableRow>
        ))}
      </TableBody>
    </Table>
  );
};

interface Resource {
  name: string;
  size: string;
  owner: string;
  status: boolean;
}

export default TableStripedExample;
```

### Small table

A table can be displayed with smaller rows.

```tsx
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { DocumentIcon, StarIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { FolderIcon, PdfIcon, PodcastIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato-mime';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableHeaderCellSortDirection,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const resources: Resource[] = Array.from({ length: 16 }).map(() => ({
  name:
    faker.system.fileName({ extensionCount: 0 }) + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['.pdf', '.mp3', '']),
  size:
    faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 600 }) + ' ' + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['KB', 'MB', 'GB']),
  owner: faker.person.fullName(),
  status: faker.datatype.boolean(),
}));

const TableSmallExample: FC = () => {
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<TableHeaderCellSortDirection>();
  const { selection, toggle, clear, isSelected, select } = useSelection<Resource>({
    mode: 'multiple',
  });

  const getIcon = (name: string): React.ReactNode => {
    switch (name.split('.').pop()) {
      case 'pdf':
        return <PdfIcon size="24px" />;
      case 'mp3':
        return <PodcastIcon size="24px" />;
      default:
        return <FolderIcon size="24px" />;
    }
  };

  const toggleSelectAll = (checked: boolean): void => {
    if (checked) {
      select(...resources);
    } else {
      clear();
    }
  };

  const sortedResources = useMemo(() => {
    if (sortDirection === 'ascending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    }

    if (sortDirection === 'descending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => b.name.localeCompare(a.name));
    }

    return resources;
  }, [sortDirection]);

  return (
    <Table style={{ width: '100%', height: 'fit-content' }} size="small">
      <TableHead>
        <TableRow>
          <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, minWidth: 40 }}>
            <div style={{ display: 'inline-flex', alignItems: 'center', height: 24 }}>
              <Checkbox
                isChecked={selection.length === resources.length}
                isIndeterminate={selection.length > 0 && selection.length < resources.length}
                onCheckedChange={toggleSelectAll}
              />
            </div>
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, lineHeight: '8px' }}>
            <DocumentIcon size="16px" />
          </TableHeaderCell>

          <TableHeaderCell
            isSortable
            sortDirection={sortDirection}
            onSortDirectionChange={setSortDirection}
          >
            Name
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell align="end" style={{ width: 68 }}>
            File size
          </TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell>Owned by</TableHeaderCell>
          <TableHeaderCell />
        </TableRow>
      </TableHead>
      <TableBody>
        {sortedResources.map(resource => (
          <TableRow
            key={resource.name}
            tabIndex={0}
            isSelected={isSelected(resource)}
            onClick={() => toggle(resource)}
            onKeyDown={e => {
              if (e.key === ' ') {
                toggle(resource);
              }
            }}
          >
            <TableCell align="center">
              <Checkbox inert isChecked={isSelected(resource)} />
            </TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>{getIcon(resource.name)}</TableCell>
            <TableCell>{resource.name}</TableCell>
            <TableCell align="end">{resource.size}</TableCell>
            <TableCell>{resource.owner}</TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>
              {resource.status && <StarIcon size="16px" />}
            </TableCell>
          </TableRow>
        ))}
      </TableBody>
    </Table>
  );
};

interface Resource {
  name: string;
  size: string;
  owner: string;
  status: boolean;
}

export default TableSmallExample;
```

### Large table

A table can be displayed with larger rows.

```tsx
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { DocumentIcon, StarIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { FolderIcon, PdfIcon, PodcastIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato-mime';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';

  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableHeaderCellSortDirection,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const resources: Resource[] = Array.from({ length: 16 }).map(() => ({
  name:
    faker.system.fileName({ extensionCount: 0 }) + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['.pdf', '.mp3', '']),

    faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 600 }) + ' ' + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['KB', 'MB', 'GB']),

            Name

            File size

      </TableHead>

          <TableRow
            key={resource.name}
            tabIndex={0}
            isSelected={isSelected(resource)}
            onClick={() => toggle(resource)}
            onKeyDown={e => {
              if (e.key === ' ') {
                toggle(resource);
              }
            }}
          >
            <TableCell align="center">
              <Checkbox inert isChecked={isSelected(resource)} />
            </TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>{getIcon(resource.name)}</TableCell>

            <TableCell>{resource.name}</TableCell>
            <TableCell align="end">{resource.size}</TableCell>
            <TableCell>{resource.owner}</TableCell>
            <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>
              {resource.status && <StarIcon size="16px" />}
            </TableCell>
          </TableRow>
        ))}
      </TableBody>
    </Table>
  );
};

interface Resource {
  name: string;
  size: string;
  owner: string;
  status: boolean;
}

export default TableLargeExample;
```

### Resizable columns

[Tanstack table](https://tanstack.com/table/latest) can be used in conjunction with the table components to provide resizable columns.

```tsx
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { ColumnDef, flexRender, getCoreRowModel, useReactTable } from '@tanstack/react-table';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

interface Person {
  id: string;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  email: string;
  city: string;
}

function makeData(count: number): Person[] {
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => ({
    id: `person-${index}`,
    firstName: faker.person.firstName(),
    lastName: faker.person.lastName(),
    email: faker.internet.email().toLowerCase(),
    city: faker.location.city(),
  }));
}

const defaultData: Person[] = makeData(16);

const defaultColumns = [
  {
    id: 'select',
    header: '',
    size: 40,
    minSize: 40,
    enableResizing: false,
    cell: ({ row }) => <Checkbox inert checked={row.getIsSelected()} />,
  },
  {
    accessorKey: 'firstName',
    header: 'First Name',
    minSize: 100,
  },
  {
    accessorKey: 'lastName',
    header: 'Last Name',
    minSize: 100,
  },
  {
    accessorKey: 'email',
    header: 'Email',
    size: 310,
    minSize: 180,
  },
  {
    accessorKey: 'city',
    header: 'City',
    minSize: 100,
  },
] as ColumnDef<Person>[];

const TableResizableExample: FC = () => {
  const [rowSelection, setRowSelection] = React.useState({});

  const table = useReactTable({
    data: defaultData,
    columns: defaultColumns,
    state: {
      rowSelection,
    },
    onRowSelectionChange: setRowSelection,
    getCoreRowModel: getCoreRowModel(),
    enableColumnResizing: true,
    columnResizeMode: 'onChange',
    enableRowSelection: true,
    enableMultiRowSelection: true,
    getRowId: row => row.id,
  });

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'block',
        width: '100%',
        overflow: 'auto',
      }}
    >
      <Table>
        <TableHead>
          <TableRow>
            {table.getHeaderGroups()[0].headers.map((header, idx, arr) => (
              <TableHeaderCell
                key={header.id}
                style={{
                  width: header.getSize(),
                  minWidth: header.getSize(),
                  maxWidth: header.getSize(),
                  position: 'relative',
                  paddingRight: '10px',
                }}
                className="header-cell truncate"
              >
                {header.id === 'select' ? (
                  <Checkbox
                    isChecked={table.getIsAllRowsSelected()}
                    isIndeterminate={table.getIsSomeRowsSelected()}
                    onCheckedChange={checked => table.toggleAllRowsSelected(!!checked)}
                  />
                ) : (
                  flexRender(header.column.columnDef.header, header.getContext())
                )}
                {header.column.getCanResize() && (
                  <div
                    onMouseDown={header.getResizeHandler()}
                    onTouchStart={header.getResizeHandler()}
                    onKeyDown={e => {
                      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
                        table.setColumnSizing(data => ({
                          ...data,
                          [header.id]: header.column.getSize() - 10,
                        }));
                        e.preventDefault();
                      } else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
                        table.setColumnSizing(data => ({
                          ...data,
                          [header.id]: header.column.getSize() + 10,
                        }));
                        e.preventDefault();
                      }
                    }}
                    className="resizer"
                    data-resizing={header.column.getIsResizing()}
                    tabIndex={0}
                    role="separator"
                    aria-orientation="horizontal"
                    aria-label="Resize column"
                  />
                )}
              </TableHeaderCell>
            ))}
          </TableRow>
        </TableHead>
        <TableBody>
          {table.getRowModel().rows.map(row => (
            <TableRow
              key={row.id}
              isSelected={row.getIsSelected()}
              tabIndex={0}
              onClick={() => row.toggleSelected()}
              onKeyDown={e => {
                if (e.key === ' ') {
                  row.toggleSelected();
                  e.preventDefault();
                }
              }}
            >
              {row.getVisibleCells().map(cell => (
                <TableCell
                  key={cell.id}
                  style={{
                    width: cell.column.getSize(),
                    minWidth: cell.column.getSize(),
                    maxWidth: cell.column.getSize(),
                  }}
                  className="truncate"
                >
                  {cell.column.id === 'select' ? (
                    <Checkbox inert checked={row.getIsSelected()} />
                  ) : (
                    flexRender(
                      cell.column.columnDef.cell ?? (() => cell.getValue()),
                      cell.getContext()
                    )
                  )}
                </TableCell>
              ))}
            </TableRow>
          ))}
        </TableBody>
      </Table>

      <style>
        {`
          .header-cell {
            position: relative;
            padding-right: 10px;
          }

          .header-cell:has(.resizer):hover {
            background: var(--jato-background-elevation-inline-content);
            box-shadow:
              inset 1px 0px 0 0 var(--jato-border-color-primary-hover),
              inset -1px 0px 0 0 var(--jato-border-color-primary-hover);
          }

          .resizer {
            position: absolute;
            right: 0;
            top: 0;
            height: 32px;
            width: 3px;
            cursor: col-resize;
            user-select: none;
            touch-action: none;
            transition: opacity 0.2s;
            z-index: 1;
            border-inline-end: 1px solid var(--uxa-table-cell__border-color);
            outline: none;
            opacity: 0;
          }

          .resizer::before {
            content: '';
            position: absolute;
            left: -12px;
            top: 0;
            width: 24px;
            height: 100%;
            cursor: col-resize;
            z-index: 2;
          }

          .resizer:hover {
            background: var(--jato-cta-background-primary-neutral);
            opacity: 1;
          }

          .resizer[data-resizing=true],
          .resizer:focus-visible {
            background: var(--jato-cta-background-primary-active);
          }

          .truncate {
            overflow: hidden;
            text-overflow: ellipsis;
            white-space: nowrap;
          }
        `}
      </style>
    </div>
  );
};

export default TableResizableExample;
```

### Virtual scrolling

For large datasets, virtual scrolling can be implemented using [TanStack Virtual](https://tanstack.com/virtual/latest) to efficiently render only the visible rows, improving performance with thousands of records.

```tsx
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { useVirtualizer } from '@tanstack/react-virtual';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef } from 'react';

interface Product {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  category: string;
  price: number;
  stock: number;
  rating: number;
}

function makeData(count: number): Product[] {
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => ({
    id: index + 1,
    name: faker.commerce.productName(),
    category: faker.commerce.department(),
    price: parseFloat(faker.commerce.price()),
    stock: faker.number.int({ min: 0, max: 1000 }),
    rating: parseFloat(faker.number.float({ min: 1, max: 5, fractionDigits: 1 }).toFixed(1)),
  }));
}

const TableVirtualExample: FC = () => {
  const tableContainerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [data] = React.useState(() => makeData(50000));
  const { selection, toggle, clear, isSelected, select } = useSelection<Product>({
    mode: 'multiple',
  });

  const rowVirtualizer = useVirtualizer({
    count: data.length,
    getScrollElement: () => tableContainerRef.current,
    estimateSize: () => 34,
    overscan: 10,
  });

  const toggleSelectAll = React.useCallback(
    (checked: boolean) => {
      if (checked) {
        select(...data);
      } else {
        clear();
      }
    },
    [data, select, clear]
  );

  return (
    <div
      ref={tableContainerRef}
      style={{
        overflow: 'auto',
        height: '566px',
        width: '100%',
      }}
    >
      <Table style={{ width: '100%', tableLayout: 'fixed' }}>
        <TableHead
          style={{
            position: 'sticky',
            top: 0,
            zIndex: 1,
            backgroundColor: 'var(--uxa-elevation__background-color--1)',
          }}
        >
          <TableRow>
            <TableHeaderCell style={{ width: 40, flex: '0 0 40px' }}>
              <Checkbox
                isChecked={selection.length === data.length}
                isIndeterminate={selection.length > 0 && selection.length < data.length}
                onCheckedChange={toggleSelectAll}
              />
            </TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell style={{ width: 60, flex: '0 0 60px' }}>ID</TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell style={{ flex: '1 1 0', minWidth: 0 }}>Product Name</TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell style={{ flex: '1 1 0', minWidth: 0 }}>Category</TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell style={{ width: 80, flex: '0 0 80px' }} align="end">
              Price
            </TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell style={{ width: 80, flex: '0 0 80px' }} align="end">
              Stock
            </TableHeaderCell>
          </TableRow>
        </TableHead>
        <TableBody
          style={{
            height: `${rowVirtualizer.getTotalSize()}px`,
            position: 'relative',
            width: '100%',
          }}
        >
          {rowVirtualizer.getVirtualItems().map(virtualRow => {
            const product = data[virtualRow.index];
            return (
              <TableRow
                key={product.id}
                data-index={virtualRow.index}
                ref={node => rowVirtualizer.measureElement(node)}
                isSelected={isSelected(product)}
                tabIndex={0}
                onClick={() => toggle(product)}
                onKeyDown={e => {
                  if (e.key === ' ') {
                    toggle(product);
                  }
                }}
                style={{
                  position: 'absolute',
                  transform: `translateY(${virtualRow.start}px)`,
                  width: '100%',
                  display: 'flex',
                }}
              >
                <TableCell
                  style={{ display: 'flex', width: 40, flex: '0 0 40px', lineHeight: '34px' }}
                >
                  <Checkbox style={{ display: 'block' }} inert isChecked={isSelected(product)} />
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell
                  style={{
                    width: 60,
                    flex: '0 0 60px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  }}
                >
                  {product.id}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell
                  style={{
                    flex: '1 1 0',
                    minWidth: 0,
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  }}
                >
                  {product.name}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell
                  style={{
                    flex: '1 1 0',
                    minWidth: 0,
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  }}
                >
                  {product.category}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell
                  style={{
                    width: 80,
                    flex: '0 0 80px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'flex-end',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  }}
                >
                  {`$${product.price.toFixed(2)}`}
                </TableCell>
                <TableCell
                  style={{
                    width: 80,
                    flex: '0 0 80px',
                    display: 'flex',
                    alignItems: 'center',
                    justifyContent: 'flex-end',
                    overflow: 'hidden',
                    textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
                    whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
                  }}
                >
                  {product.stock.toLocaleString()}
                </TableCell>
              </TableRow>
            );
          })}
        </TableBody>
      </Table>
    </div>
  );
};

export default TableVirtualExample;
```

### Drag and drop row reordering

[DndKit](https://dndkit.com/) can be used in conjunction with the table components to provide drag and drop row reordering.

```tsx
import {
  DndContext,
  KeyboardSensor,
  MouseSensor,
  TouchSensor,
  pointerWithin,
  useSensor,
  useSensors,
  type DragEndEvent,
} from '@dnd-kit/core';
import { restrictToVerticalAxis } from '@dnd-kit/modifiers';
import {
  SortableContext,
  arrayMove,
  useSortable,
  verticalListSortingStrategy,
} from '@dnd-kit/sortable';
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { mergeProps, mergeRefs } from '@react-aria/utils';
import '@ux-aspects-universal/react/a11y';
import { FocusKeyManager, useTabbable } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/a11y';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { DragHandleIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import * as React from 'react';
import { CSSProperties, FC, useRef } from 'react';
import { flushSync } from 'react-dom';

interface Person {
  id: string;
  firstName: string;
  lastName: string;
  email: string;
}

function makeData(count: number): Person[] {
  return Array.from({ length: count }, (_, index) => ({
    id: `row-${index}`,
    firstName: faker.person.firstName(),
    lastName: faker.person.lastName(),
    email: faker.internet.email().toLowerCase(),
  }));
}

const DraggableTableRow = React.memo(
  ({
    row,
    isSelected,
    onToggle,
    onMoveRow,
  }: {
    row: Person;
    isSelected: boolean;
    onToggle: () => void;
    onMoveRow: (rowId: string, direction: number) => void;
  }): JSX.Element => {
    const ref = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
    const { attributes, isDragging, listeners, setNodeRef, transform, transition } = useSortable({
      id: row.id,
    });
    const { tabbableProps, tabIndex, setActive } = useTabbable(ref, { id: row.id });

    const style: CSSProperties = {
      opacity: isDragging ? 0.8 : 1,
      position: 'relative',
      transform: transform ? `translate(${transform.x}px, ${transform.y}px)` : undefined,
      transition,
      zIndex: isDragging ? 1000 : 0,
    };

    const { onKeyDown, ...focusProps } = tabbableProps;

    const handleKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLElement>): void => {
      // Use Cmd key on Mac, Ctrl on Windows/Linux
      const modifierKey = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;

      if (modifierKey && e.key === 'ArrowUp') {
        e.preventDefault();
        flushSync(() => onMoveRow(row.id, -1));
        setActive();
      } else if (modifierKey && e.key === 'ArrowDown') {
        e.preventDefault();
        flushSync(() => onMoveRow(row.id, 1));
        setActive();
      } else if (e.key === ' ') {
        onToggle();
        e.preventDefault();
      } else {
        onKeyDown?.(e);
      }
    };

    return (
      <TableRow
        ref={mergeRefs(setNodeRef, ref)}
        style={style}
        {...mergeProps(
          {
            isSelected,
            tabIndex,
            onClick: onToggle,
            onKeyDown: handleKeyDown,
          },
          focusProps
        )}
      >
        <TableCell style={{ width: 24, textAlign: 'center' }}>
          <div
            {...attributes}
            {...listeners}
            style={{
              cursor: 'move',
              borderRadius: '4px',
              padding: '4px',
              backgroundColor: 'transparent',
              border: 'none',
            }}
            onClick={e => e.stopPropagation()} // Prevent row selection when dragging
          >
            <DragHandleIcon size="16px" />
          </div>
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell align="center" style={{ width: 40 }}>
          <Checkbox inert isChecked={isSelected} />
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell>
          <span
            style={{
              display: 'block',
              whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
              overflow: 'hidden',
              textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
            }}
          >
            {row.firstName}
          </span>
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell>
          <span
            style={{
              display: 'block',
              whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
              overflow: 'hidden',
              textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
            }}
          >
            {row.lastName}
          </span>
        </TableCell>
        <TableCell>
          <span
            style={{
              display: 'block',
              whiteSpace: 'nowrap',
              overflow: 'hidden',
              textOverflow: 'ellipsis',
            }}
          >
            {row.email}
          </span>
        </TableCell>
      </TableRow>
    );
  }
);

const TableRowDragAndDropExample: FC = () => {
  const [data, setData] = React.useState(() => makeData(16));
  const { selection, toggle, clear, isSelected, select } = useSelection<Person>({
    mode: 'multiple',
  });

  // Reorder rows after drag & drop
  function handleDragEnd(event: DragEndEvent): void {
    const { active, over } = event;
    if (active && over && active.id !== over.id) {
      setData(currentData => {
        const oldIndex = currentData.findIndex(item => item.id === active.id);
        const newIndex = currentData.findIndex(item => item.id === over.id);
        return arrayMove(currentData, oldIndex, newIndex);
      });
    }
  }

  // Move row up or down with keyboard
  const moveRow = React.useCallback((rowId: string, direction: number): void => {
    setData(currentData => {
      const currentIndex = currentData.findIndex(r => r.id === rowId);
      const newIndex = currentIndex + direction;
      if (newIndex < 0 || newIndex >= currentData.length) {
        return currentData;
      }
      return arrayMove(currentData, currentIndex, newIndex);
    });
  }, []);

  const toggleSelectAll = (checked: boolean): void => {
    if (checked) {
      select(...data);
    } else {
      clear();
    }
  };

  const sensors = useSensors(
    useSensor(MouseSensor, {
      activationConstraint: {
        distance: 8,
      },
    }),
    useSensor(TouchSensor, {
      activationConstraint: {
        delay: 200,
        tolerance: 8,
      },
    }),
    useSensor(KeyboardSensor)
  );

  const rowIds = React.useMemo(() => data.map(row => row.id), [data]);

  return (
    <FocusKeyManager orientation="vertical">
      <Table style={{ tableLayout: 'fixed', width: '100%' }}>
        <TableHead>
          <TableRow>
            <TableHeaderCell style={{ width: 24 }} />
            <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, minWidth: 40 }}>
              <Checkbox
                isChecked={selection.length === data.length}
                isIndeterminate={selection.length > 0 && selection.length < data.length}
                onCheckedChange={toggleSelectAll}
              />
            </TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell>First Name</TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell>Last Name</TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell>Email</TableHeaderCell>
          </TableRow>
        </TableHead>
        <TableBody>
          <DndContext
            collisionDetection={pointerWithin}
            modifiers={[restrictToVerticalAxis]}
            onDragEnd={handleDragEnd}
            sensors={sensors}
          >
            <SortableContext items={rowIds} strategy={verticalListSortingStrategy}>
              {data.map(row => (
                <DraggableTableRow
                  key={row.id}
                  row={row}
                  isSelected={isSelected(row)}
                  onToggle={() => toggle(row)}
                  onMoveRow={moveRow}
                />
              ))}
            </SortableContext>
          </DndContext>
        </TableBody>
      </Table>
    </FocusKeyManager>
  );
};

export default TableRowDragAndDropExample;
```

---

Navigation Tabs provide a way to logically organize content associated with one object, work space or screen. Navigation Tabs can help to simplify the UI and help navigate to desired information.

```tsx
import { Tab, TabGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/tabs';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const TabsExample: FC = () => {
  return (
    <TabGroup>
      <Tab label="Overview">
        <p style={{ marginBlock: '1rem' }}>
          UX Aspects Universal is a UI component library with a focus on accessibility,
          customization, and developer experience. Whether you're building an application from
          scratch or looking to enhance an existing one, our components are here to streamline the
          process and boost your productivity.
        </p>
      </Tab>

      <Tab label="Versions">
        <p style={{ marginBlock: '1rem' }}>
          UX Aspects Universal is designed to work seamlessly across modern, evergreen browsers such
          as Chrome, Firefox and Edge.
        </p>

        <p>Our components target the latest two major versions of Angular and any React 16+.</p>
      </Tab>
    </TabGroup>
  );
};

export default TabsExample;
```

## Import

Import `TabGroup` to use the component.

```ts
import { TabGroup, Tab } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/tabs';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/tabs`:

### TabGroup

The `TabGroup` component is a container for tab buttons and tab content.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `activeIndex` | `number` | - | No | The index of the active tab. |
| `activeId` | `string` | - | No | The id of the active tab. |
| `orientation` | `'horizontal' | 'vertical'` | `horizontal` | No | The orientation of the tab group. |
| `onActiveIndexChange` | `(index: number) => void` | - | No | Emit whenever a new tab is active. |
| `onActiveIdChange` | `(id: string) => void` | - | No | Emit whenever a new tab is active. |

### Tab

The `Tab` component represents a single tab within a tab group.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `id` | `string` | - | No | The id of the tab. |
| `label` | `string | ReactNode` | - | Yes | The label for the tab. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the tab is disabled. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Tabs component in different scenarios.

### Vertical Tabs

Tabs can be displayed vertically by setting the `orientation` input to `'vertical'`.

```tsx
import { Tab, TabGroup } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/tabs';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC } from 'react';

const VerticalTabsExample: FC = () => {
  return (
    <TabGroup orientation="vertical">
      <Tab label="Overview">
        <p>
          UX Aspects Universal is a UI component library with a focus on accessibility,
          customization, and developer experience. Whether you're building an application from
          scratch or looking to enhance an existing one, our components are here to streamline the
          process and boost your productivity.
        </p>
      </Tab>

      <Tab label="Versions">
        <p>
          UX Aspects Universal is designed to work seamlessly across modern, evergreen browsers such
          as Chrome, Firefox and Edge.
        </p>

        <p>Our components target the latest two major versions of Angular and any React 16+.</p>
      </Tab>
    </TabGroup>
  );
};

export default VerticalTabsExample;
```

### Tabs with Icons

The content of tab labels can be customized to include icons.

Tab         | Moves focus into the tab list.                                 |
| Arrow Left  | Moves focus to the previous tab and activates it (horizontal). |
| Arrow Right | Moves focus to the next tab and activates it (horizontal).     |
| Arrow Up    | Moves focus to the previous tab and activates it (vertical).   |
| Arrow Down  | Moves focus to the next tab and activates it (vertical).       |
| Home        | Moves focus to the first tab.                                  |
| End         | Moves focus to the last tab.                                   |

## API Reference

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `size` | `small | medium | large` | `medium` | No | Define the size of the input group. |
| `leadingAddon` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Add leading addons to the input group. |
| `trailingAddon` | `ReactNode` | - | No | Add trailing addons to the input group. |

---

Toasts are short, passive messages to provide feedback relevant to the current task the user is performing. They are shown briefly at the bottom of the screen for events such as successful actions or status updates.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  NotificationContent,
  NotificationDismissButton,
  NotificationToastLayout,
  UxaNotificationRef,
  useNotification,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/notification';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef } from 'react';

const ToastExampleContent: FC = () => (
  <NotificationToastLayout>
    <NotificationContent>Toast message to give brief feedback to the user.</NotificationContent>
    <NotificationDismissButton aria-label="Dismiss notification" />
  </NotificationToastLayout>
);

const ToastExample: FC = () => {
  const { openToast } = useNotification();
  const toastRef = useRef<UxaNotificationRef>();
  const [isToastOpen, setIsToastOpen] = React.useState<boolean>(false);

  const showToast = (): void => {
    setIsToastOpen(true);
    toastRef.current = openToast(<ToastExampleContent />, { duration: 5000 });
    toastRef.current.onDismiss(() => setIsToastOpen(false));
  };

  return (
    <Button primary onClick={showToast} isDisabled={isToastOpen}>
      Show toast
    </Button>
  );
};

export default ToastExample;
```

## Import

Import the `UxaNotificationModule` to use the Toast component.

```ts
import {
  useNotification,
  NotificationContainer,
  NotificationToastLayout,
  NotificationDismissButton,
  NotificationToastActions,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/notification';
```

## Usage

At the root of your application, wrap your application content with the `NotificationContainer` component. This component provides the notification context to all descendant components — any component that calls `useNotification` must be rendered as a descendant of `NotificationContainer`. Toasts will appear overlaid on the wrapped content.

The `useNotification` hook can be used to launch and dismiss toasts. The following functions are provided by the `useNotification` hook:

| Name         | Type                                                                         | Description         |
| ------------ | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------- | ------------------- |
| `openToast`  | `(component: ReactNode, config?: NotificationConfig) => UxaNotificationRef;` | Launch a toast.     |
| `dismissAll` | `() => void;`                                                                | Dismiss all toasts. |

The `NotificationConfig` interface is documented in the Interfaces section below.

When a toast is launched, the `openToast` function will return a `UxaNotificationRef` instance which has the following methods:

| Name        | Type                             | Description                                                                        |
| ----------- | -------------------------------- | ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `dismiss`   | `() => void`                     | Dismiss this toast.                                                                |
| `onDismiss` | `(callback: () => void) => void` | Register a callback function that will be called whenever this toast is dismissed. |

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/notification`:

### NotificationToastLayout

The Toast component.

- Selector: `uxa-notification-toast-layout`

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `dismissAll` | `() => void` | - | - | Dismiss all toasts. |
| `openToast` | `(componentOrTemplateRef:  Component | TemplateRef, config?: NotificationConfig
): UxaNotificationRef
` | - | - | The method to call to open the Toast component. |

### NotificationContent

The content of the toast.

### NotificationDismissButton

The dismiss button of the toast.

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Toast component in different scenarios.

### Success Toast

The success toast is used to display a success message.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { SuccessIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/micro-focus';
import {
  NotificationContent,
  NotificationDismissButton,
  NotificationToastLayout,
  UxaNotificationRef,
  useNotification,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/notification';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef } from 'react';

const ToastSuccessExampleContent: FC = () => (
  <NotificationToastLayout icon={<SuccessIcon />} status="success">
    <NotificationContent>Toast message to give brief feedback to the user.</NotificationContent>
    <NotificationDismissButton aria-label="Dismiss notification" />
  </NotificationToastLayout>
);

const ToastSuccessExample: FC = () => {
  const { openToast } = useNotification();
  const toastRef = useRef<UxaNotificationRef>();
  const [isToastOpen, setIsToastOpen] = React.useState<boolean>(false);

  const showToast = (): void => {
    setIsToastOpen(true);
    toastRef.current = openToast(<ToastSuccessExampleContent />, { duration: 5000 });
    toastRef.current.onDismiss(() => setIsToastOpen(false));
  };

  return (
    <Button primary onClick={showToast} isDisabled={isToastOpen}>
      Show toast
    </Button>
  );
};

export default ToastSuccessExample;
```

### Progress Toast

The progress toast is used to display a progress message.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  NotificationContent,
  NotificationDismissButton,
  NotificationToastLayout,
  UxaNotificationRef,
  useNotification,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/notification';
import { Spinner } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/spinner';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef } from 'react';

const ToastProgressExampleContent: FC = () => (
  <NotificationToastLayout icon={<Spinner size="16px" />}>
    <NotificationContent>Toast message to give brief feedback to the user.</NotificationContent>
    <NotificationDismissButton aria-label="Dismiss notification" />
  </NotificationToastLayout>
);

const ToastProgressExample: FC = () => {
  const { openToast } = useNotification();
  const toastRef = useRef<UxaNotificationRef>();
  const [isToastOpen, setIsToastOpen] = React.useState<boolean>(false);

  const showToast = (): void => {
    setIsToastOpen(true);
    toastRef.current = openToast(<ToastProgressExampleContent />, { duration: 5000 });
    toastRef.current.onDismiss(() => setIsToastOpen(false));
  };

  return (
    <Button primary onClick={showToast} isDisabled={isToastOpen}>
      Show toast
    </Button>
  );
};

export default ToastProgressExample;
```

### Toast with actions

The Toast component can contain actions. These shouldn't have a duration set to keyboard and screen reader users can navigate to the action.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import {
  NotificationContent,
  NotificationDismissButton,
  NotificationToastActions,
  NotificationToastLayout,
  UxaNotificationRef,
  useNotification,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/notification';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useRef } from 'react';

const ToastExampleContent: FC = () => (
  <NotificationToastLayout>
    <NotificationContent>Toast message to give brief feedback to the user.</NotificationContent>
    <NotificationToastActions>
      <Button flat size="small">
        Undo
      </Button>
    </NotificationToastActions>
    <NotificationDismissButton aria-label="Dismiss notification" />
  </NotificationToastLayout>
);

const ToastActionsExample: FC = () => {
  const { openToast } = useNotification();
  const toastRef = useRef<UxaNotificationRef>();
  const [isToastOpen, setIsToastOpen] = React.useState<boolean>(false);

  const showToast = (): void => {
    setIsToastOpen(true);
    toastRef.current = openToast(<ToastExampleContent />, { duration: 5000 });
    toastRef.current.onDismiss(() => setIsToastOpen(false));
  };

  return (
    <Button primary onClick={showToast} isDisabled={isToastOpen}>
      Show toast
    </Button>
  );
};

export default ToastActionsExample;
```

A toggle switch is used to switch between two opposite states, such as: On / Off.

```tsx
import { ToggleSwitch } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/toggle-switch';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const ToggleSwitchExample: FC = () => {
  const [selected, setSelected] = useState(false);

  return <ToggleSwitch isSelected={selected} onValueChange={setSelected} />;
};

export default ToggleSwitchExample;
```

## Import

Import `ToggleSwitch` to use the component.

```ts
import { ToggleSwitch } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/toggle-switch';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/toggle-switch`:

### ToggleSwitch

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `value` | `string` | - | No | The value of the input element, used when submitting an HTML form. See [MDN](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/HTML/Element/input#htmlattrdefvalue). |
| `isSelected` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the element should be selected (controlled). |
| `defaultSelected` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the element should be selected (uncontrolled). |
| `onValueChange` | `(value: boolean) => void` | - | No | Emits whenever the selection state changes. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Whether the input is disabled. |
| `labelPosition` | `before | after` | - | No | The position of the label relative to the toggle switch. |

## Examples
