---

A toolbar group organizes sets of controls to support the functionality within a data table, editing area (e.g. formatting options) or a preview (e.g. for zoom and paging).

In the example shown, a table header toolbar is used above a data table and an action toolbar will be shown when one or more rows in the data table are selected.

```tsx
import { faker } from '@faker-js/faker';
import { mergeProps, mergeRefs } from '@react-aria/utils';
import { IconButton, Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { Checkbox } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/checkbox';
import { Counter } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/counter';
import {
  DocumentIcon,
  FilterIcon,
  AddIcon,
  CopyIcon,
  MoreHorizontalIcon,
  ChevronSmallDownIcon,
  AviatorPrimaryIcon,
  TreeViewIcon,
  ShowToolbarIcon,
  ViewDescriptionsIcon,
  SettingsIcon,
  UploadIcon,
  PropertiesIcon,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { FolderIcon, PdfIcon, PodcastIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato-mime';
import { VisibilityIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/micro-focus';
import { Menu, MenuItem, useMenuTrigger } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/menu';
import { Panel } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/panel';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import {
  Table,
  TableBody,
  TableCell,
  TableHead,
  TableHeaderCell,
  TableHeaderCellSortDirection,
  TableRow,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/table';
import {
  Toolbar,
  ToolbarGroup,
  ToolbarStart,
  ToolbarCenter,
  ToolbarEnd,
  ToolbarButtonGroup,
  ToolbarItem,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/toolbar';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo, useState } from 'react';

const resources: Resource[] = Array.from({ length: 16 }).map(() => ({
  name:
    faker.system.fileName({ extensionCount: 0 }) + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['.pdf', '.mp3', '']),
  size:
    faker.number.int({ min: 1, max: 600 }) + ' ' + faker.helpers.arrayElement(['KB', 'MB', 'GB']),
  owner: faker.person.fullName(),
}));

const TableHeaderExample: FC = () => {
  const [sortDirection, setSortDirection] = useState<TableHeaderCellSortDirection>();
  const { selection, toggle, clear, isSelected, select } = useSelection<Resource>({
    mode: 'multiple',
  });

  const getIcon = (name: string): React.ReactNode => {
    switch (name.split('.').pop()) {
      case 'pdf':
        return <PdfIcon size="24px" />;
      case 'mp3':
        return <PodcastIcon size="24px" />;
      default:
        return <FolderIcon size="24px" />;
    }
  };
  const toggleSelectAll = (checked: boolean): void => {
    if (checked) {
      select(...resources);
    } else {
      clear();
    }
  };

  const sortedResources = useMemo(() => {
    if (sortDirection === 'ascending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
    }

    if (sortDirection === 'descending') {
      return [...resources].sort((a, b) => b.name.localeCompare(a.name));
    }

    return resources;
  }, [sortDirection]);

  const { menuProps, triggerProps } = useMenuTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>();
  const { menuProps: selectionMenuProps, triggerProps: selectionTriggerProps } =
    useMenuTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>();

  const isSelectionToolbarCollapsed = selection.length === 0;

  const [manuallyExpanded, setManuallyExpanded] = useState(false);
  const isHeaderToolbarCollapsed = !isSelectionToolbarCollapsed && !manuallyExpanded;

  const toggleHeaderToolbar = (): void => {
    setManuallyExpanded(prev => !prev);
  };

  if (manuallyExpanded && selection.length === 0) {
    setManuallyExpanded(false);
  }

  return (
    <Panel
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        padding: 0,
        overflow: 'hidden',
        width: '100%',
      }}
    >
      <ToolbarGroup style={{ width: '100%' }}>
        <Toolbar isCollapsed={isHeaderToolbarCollapsed}>
          <ToolbarStart>
            <ToolbarButtonGroup>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton {...iconButton} secondary flat size="small" icon={<FilterIcon />} />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton {...iconButton} secondary flat size="small" icon={<AddIcon />} />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton {...iconButton} secondary flat size="small" icon={<TreeViewIcon />} />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton {...iconButton} secondary flat size="small" icon={<UploadIcon />} />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton {...iconButton} secondary flat size="small" icon={<CopyIcon />} />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
            </ToolbarButtonGroup>
          </ToolbarStart>
          <ToolbarCenter>
            <ToolbarItem>
              {button => {
                const { ref, ...restProps } = button;
                const { ref: triggerRef, ...restTriggerProps } = triggerProps;
                return (
                  <Button
                    {...mergeProps(restProps, restTriggerProps)}
                    ref={mergeRefs(ref, triggerRef)}
                    flat
                    secondary
                    iconBefore={<FolderIcon size="24px" />}
                    iconAfter={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />}
                  >
                    <h2 style={{ margin: 0 }}>Container title</h2>
                  </Button>
                );
              }}
            </ToolbarItem>
            <Menu {...menuProps}>
              <MenuItem>Container action 1</MenuItem>
              <MenuItem>Container action 2</MenuItem>
              <MenuItem>Container action 3</MenuItem>
            </Menu>
          </ToolbarCenter>
          <ToolbarEnd>
            <ToolbarButtonGroup>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton
                    {...iconButton}
                    secondary
                    flat
                    size="small"
                    icon={<AviatorPrimaryIcon />}
                  />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton
                    {...iconButton}
                    secondary
                    flat
                    size="small"
                    icon={<ViewDescriptionsIcon />}
                  />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton
                    {...iconButton}
                    secondary
                    flat
                    size="small"
                    icon={<VisibilityIcon />}
                  />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton {...iconButton} secondary flat size="small" icon={<SettingsIcon />} />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton
                    {...iconButton}
                    secondary
                    flat
                    size="small"
                    icon={<MoreHorizontalIcon />}
                  />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
            </ToolbarButtonGroup>
          </ToolbarEnd>
        </Toolbar>
        <Toolbar isCollapsed={isSelectionToolbarCollapsed}>
          <ToolbarStart>
            <ToolbarButtonGroup>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {button => {
                  const { ref, ...restProps } = button;
                  const { ref: triggerRef, ...restTriggerProps } = selectionTriggerProps;
                  return (
                    <Button
                      {...mergeProps(restProps, restTriggerProps)}
                      ref={mergeRefs(ref, triggerRef)}
                      flat
                      secondary
                      iconBefore={<Counter>{selection.length}</Counter>}
                      iconAfter={<ChevronSmallDownIcon />}
                    >
                      Selected
                    </Button>
                  );
                }}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <Menu {...selectionMenuProps}>
                {selection.map(item => (
                  <MenuItem key={item.name}>{item.name}</MenuItem>
                ))}
              </Menu>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton
                    {...iconButton}
                    secondary
                    flat
                    size="small"
                    icon={<PropertiesIcon />}
                  />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>

              <ToolbarItem>
                {button => (
                  <Button {...button} secondary flat size="small">
                    Action 1
                  </Button>
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {button => (
                  <Button {...button} secondary flat size="small">
                    Action 2
                  </Button>
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {button => (
                  <Button {...button} secondary flat size="small">
                    Action 3
                  </Button>
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {button => (
                  <Button {...button} secondary flat size="small">
                    Action 4
                  </Button>
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
              <ToolbarItem>
                {iconButton => (
                  <IconButton
                    {...iconButton}
                    secondary
                    flat
                    size="small"
                    icon={<MoreHorizontalIcon />}
                  />
                )}
              </ToolbarItem>
            </ToolbarButtonGroup>
          </ToolbarStart>
          <ToolbarEnd>
            <ToolbarItem isCollapsed={!isHeaderToolbarCollapsed}>
              {iconButton => (
                <IconButton
                  {...iconButton}
                  secondary
                  flat
                  size="small"
                  onClick={toggleHeaderToolbar}
                  icon={<ShowToolbarIcon />}
                />
              )}
            </ToolbarItem>
          </ToolbarEnd>
        </Toolbar>
      </ToolbarGroup>
      <Table style={{ width: '100%' }}>
        <TableHead>
          <TableRow>
            <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, minWidth: 40 }}>
              <Checkbox
                isChecked={selection.length === resources.length}
                isIndeterminate={selection.length > 0 && selection.length < resources.length}
                onCheckedChange={toggleSelectAll}
              />
            </TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell align="center" style={{ width: 40, lineHeight: '8px' }}>
              <DocumentIcon size="16px" />
            </TableHeaderCell>

            <TableHeaderCell
              isSortable
              sortDirection={sortDirection}
              onSortDirectionChange={setSortDirection}
            >
              Name
            </TableHeaderCell>
            <TableHeaderCell>Owned by</TableHeaderCell>
          </TableRow>
        </TableHead>
        <TableBody>
          {sortedResources.map(resource => (
            <TableRow
              key={resource.name}
              tabIndex={0}
              isSelected={isSelected(resource)}
              onClick={() => toggle(resource)}
              onKeyDown={e => {
                if (e.key === ' ') {
                  toggle(resource);
                }
              }}
            >
              <TableCell align="center">
                <Checkbox inert isChecked={isSelected(resource)} />
              </TableCell>
              <TableCell style={{ lineHeight: '8px' }}>{getIcon(resource.name)}</TableCell>

              <TableCell>{resource.name}</TableCell>
              <TableCell>{resource.owner}</TableCell>
            </TableRow>
          ))}
        </TableBody>
      </Table>
    </Panel>
  );
};

export default TableHeaderExample;

interface Resource {
  name: string;
  size: string;
  owner: string;
}
```

## Import

Import `Toolbar` to use the component.

```ts
import { Toolbar } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/toolbar';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/toolbar`:

### ToolbarGroup

The `ToolbarGroup` is a wrapper that groups together multiple `Toolbar` components. Primarily used to present header information and enable interactive capabilities within data tables.

### Toolbar

The `Toolbar` component is a container that provides a set of controls for actions or settings.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isCollapsed` | `boolean` | `false` | No | Whether the toolbar should be hidden. |

### ToolbarStart

The `ToolbarStart` is a container to align the contents of the toolbar to the start of the writing direction.

### ToolbarCenter

The `ToolbarCenter` is a container to center align the contents in the toolbar.

### ToolbarEnd

The `ToolbarEnd` is a container to align the contents of the toolbar to the end of the writing direction.

### ToolbarButtonGroup

The `ToolbarButtonGroup` is a component which groups a set of buttons.

### ToolbarItem

The `ToolbarItem` is a container for interactive elements within a toolbar to manage accessibility and collapsed states.

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `children` | `(props: ToolbarItemProps) => ReactNode` | - | - | Render function receiving props to spread onto the toolbar button for focus management and keyboard navigation. |
| `isCollapsed` | `boolean` | - | No | Toggled state of a toolbar button. |

---

Tooltips show brief and informative messages in a relation to a specific component, when the user hovers or focuses on them.

[UXD guidelines](https://uxd.opentext.net/guidelines/tooltips.htm)

## Import

Import `Tooltip` to use the component.

```ts
import { Tooltip } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/popover';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/popover`:

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `isOpen` | `boolean` | - | No | Determine if the Tooltip is open or closed. |
| `placement` | `above | below | before | after` | `below` | No | Where the Tooltip is positioned vertically relative to the trigger. |
| `variant` | `auto | light | dark` | `auto` | No | The color variant of the Tooltip. |
| `triggerRef` | `RefObject` | - | Yes | The element used to trigger Tooltip. The Tooltip will be positioned relative to this element. |
| `openTriggers` | `click | mouseenter | focus` | `['mouseenter', 'focus']` | No | Events that will cause the Tooltip to appear. |
| `closeTriggers` | `click | mouseleave | blur | escape | clickoutside | mouseoutside` | `['mouseleave', 'blur']` | No | Events that will cause the Tooltip to hide. |
| `onOpenChange` | `(isOpen: boolean) => void` | - | No | Emit whenever the Tooltip is opened or closed. |
| `isDisabled` | `boolean` | - | No | Determine if the Tooltip is disabled. |
| `offset` | `number` | - | No | The offset of the tooltip from the trigger element. |
| `delay` | `number` | - | No | The delay before the tooltip is shown in milliseconds. |
| `container` | `HTMLElement` | - | No | The container element the tooltip will be appended to. This defaults to the document body. |

## Examples

### Placement

The tooltip component supports four placement options: above, below, before, and after.

```tsx
import { Button } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { Placement, Tooltip, useTooltipTrigger } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/popover';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const TooltipButton: FC<{ label: string; placement: Placement }> = ({ label, placement }) => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(false);
  const { tooltipProps, triggerProps } = useTooltipTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>({
    isOpen,
    onOpenChange: setIsOpen,
  });

  return (
    <>
      <Button primary {...triggerProps}>
        {label}
      </Button>
      <Tooltip {...tooltipProps} placement={placement}>
        <span>Tooltip {placement}</span>
      </Tooltip>
    </>
  );
};

const TooltipPlacementExample: FC = () => {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: 'flex',
        flexDirection: 'column',
        alignItems: 'center',
        gap: 40,
      }}
    >
      <TooltipButton label="Above" placement="above" />
      <div style={{ display: 'flex', gap: '150px' }}>
        <TooltipButton label="Before" placement="before" />
        <TooltipButton label="After" placement="after" />
      </div>
      <TooltipButton label="Below" placement="below" />
    </div>
  );
};

export default TooltipPlacementExample;
```

### Tooltip with icon button

The tooltip component supports icon button.

```tsx
import { IconButton } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/button';
import { NotificationIcon } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { Tooltip, useTooltipTrigger } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/popover';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useState } from 'react';

const TooltipIconButtonExample: FC = () => {
  const [isOpen, setIsOpen] = useState(true);

  const { tooltipProps, triggerProps } = useTooltipTrigger<HTMLButtonElement>({
    isOpen,
    onOpenChange: setIsOpen,
  });

  return (
    <>
      <IconButton
        primary
        type="button"
        {...triggerProps}
        aria-label="notification button"
        icon={<NotificationIcon />}
      />
      <Tooltip {...tooltipProps}>
        <span>Notification icon button</span>
      </Tooltip>
    </>
  );
};

export default TooltipIconButtonExample;
```

---

A tree control displays the contents of a hierarchical object structure within the system.

```tsx
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import { Tree, TreeNodeModel, useTreeControl } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/tree';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo } from 'react';

const TreeExample: FC = () => {
  const items = useMemo<TreeNodeModel[]>(
    () => [
      {
        title: 'Dashboard',
        children: [
          {
            title: 'Sales',
          },
        ],
      },
      {
        title: 'Products',
        children: [
          {
            title: 'Listview',
          },
          {
            title: 'Partition Map',
          },
          {
            title: 'Social Chart',
            children: [
              {
                title: 'Details',
              },
              {
                title: 'Specification',
              },
              {
                title: 'Support',
              },
            ],
          },
          {
            title: 'Sankey Chart',
          },
        ],
      },
      {
        title: 'Settings',
      },
      {
        title: 'Account',
      },
    ],
    []
  );

  const treeControl = useTreeControl({ items });
  const selectionManager = useSelection({ mode: 'multiple' });

  return <Tree treeControl={treeControl} selectionManager={selectionManager} />;
};

export default TreeExample;
```

## Import

Import `Tree` to use the component.

```ts
import { Tree } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/tree';
```

## API Reference

The following exports are available within `@ux-aspects-universal/react/tree`:

| Property | Type | Default | Required | Description |
| -------- | ---- | ------- | -------- | ----------- |
| `node` | `FC
>` | - | No | Custom component used to render nodes. |
| `treeControl` | `UxaTreeControl
` | - | Yes | Provides data and can be used to programmatically expand/collapse nodes. |
| `selectionManager` | `UxaSelectionManager
| undefined` | - | Yes | An instance of SelectionManager. If provided, nodes will be selectable. |
| `loadChildren` | `(item: TreeNodeModel
) => Promise` | - | No | Load children asynchronously. |

## Examples

The following examples demonstrate how to use the Tree component in different scenarios.

### Async Loading

Lazy load the children of a node whenever it is expanded. This can be useful when working with large datasets.

```tsx
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import { Tree, TreeNodeModel, useTreeControl } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/tree';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useCallback, useState } from 'react';

const TreeAsyncExample: FC = () => {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<TreeNodeModel[]>([
    { title: 'Dashboard', hasChildren: true },
    { title: 'Products', hasChildren: true },
    { title: 'Settings' },
    { title: 'Account' },
  ]);

  const treeControl = useTreeControl({ items });
  const selectionManager = useSelection({ mode: 'multiple' });

  const loadChildren = useCallback(
    async (node: TreeNodeModel) => {
      if (node.title === 'Dashboard') {
        node.children = [{ title: 'Sales' }];
      }

      if (node.title === 'Products') {
        node.children = [
          { title: 'Listview', hasChildren: true },
          { title: 'Partition Map' },
          { title: 'Social Chart' },
        ];
      }

      if (node.title === 'Listview') {
        node.children = [{ title: 'Listview 1' }, { title: 'Listview 2' }, { title: 'Listview 3' }];
      }

      // simulate async loading
      await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 1500));

      // update the tree items
      setItems([...items]);
    },
    [items]
  );

  return (
    <Tree
      treeControl={treeControl}
      selectionManager={selectionManager}
      loadChildren={loadChildren}
    />
  );
};

export default TreeAsyncExample;
```

### Tree Node Icons

Add icons to tree nodes to provide additional information about what the node represents.

```tsx
import {
  ChartLineIcon,
  ChartPartitionIcon,
  ChartSankeyIcon,
  DashboardIcon,
  LaunchIcon,
  ListIcon,
  SettingsIcon,
  ShareIcon,
  UserIcon,
} from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/icon/jato';
import { useSelection } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/selection';
import { Tree, TreeNodeModel, useTreeControl } from '@ux-aspects-universal/react/tree';
import * as React from 'react';
import { FC, useMemo } from 'react';

const TreeIconExample: FC = () => {
  const items = useMemo<TreeNodeModel[]>(
    () => [
      {
        title: 'Dashboard',
        icon: <DashboardIcon />,
        children: [
          {
            title: 'Sales',
            icon: <ChartLineIcon />,
          },
        ],
      },
      {
        title: 'Products',
        icon: <LaunchIcon />,
        children: [
          { title: 'Listview', icon: <ListIcon /> },
          { title: 'Partition Map', icon: <ChartPartitionIcon /> },
          {
            title: 'Social Chart',
            icon: <ShareIcon />,
            children: [
              {
                title: 'Details',
                icon: <ShareIcon />,
              },
              {
                title: 'Specification',
                icon: <ShareIcon />,
              },
              {
                title: 'Support',
                icon: <ShareIcon />,
              },
            ],
          },
          { title: 'Sankey Chart', icon: <ChartSankeyIcon /> },
        ],
      },

      {
        title: 'Settings',
        icon: <SettingsIcon />,
      },
      {
        title: 'Account',
        icon: <UserIcon />,
      },
    ],
    []
  );

  const treeControl = useTreeControl({ items });
  const selectionManager = useSelection({ mode: 'multiple' });

  return <Tree treeControl={treeControl} selectionManager={selectionManager} />;
};

export default TreeIconExample;
```
